**44** Then he told them, "These are the words that I spoke to you while I was still with you that everything written about me in the Law of Moses, the Prophets, and the Psalms had to be fulfilled."

**45** Then he opened their minds so that they might understand the Scriptures.

**46** He told them, "This is how it is written: the Messiah <sup>&#123;Or Christ&#125;</sup> was to suffer and rise from the dead on the third day,

**47** and then repentance and forgiveness of sins is to be proclaimed in his name to all the nations, beginning at Jerusalem.

**48** You are witnesses of these things.

**49** I am sending to you what my Father promised, so stay here in the city until you have been clothed with power from on high."

**50** <sup>&#123;Jesus is Taken up to Heaven&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Mark 16:19-20; Acts 1:9-11)&#125;</sup> d Later, he led them out as far as Bethany, lifted up his hands, and blessed them.

**51** While he was blessing them, he left them and was taken up to heaven. <sup>&#123;Other mss. lack and was taken up to heaven&#125;</sup>

**52** They worshipped him and <sup>&#123;Other mss. lack worshipped him and&#125;</sup> returned to Jerusalem filled with great joy.

**53** They were continually in the Temple, blessing <sup>&#123;Other mss. read praising ; still other mss. read praising and blessing&#125;</sup> God. <sup>&#123;Other mss. read God. Amen.&#125;</sup>

---

# Mark (ISV)

## Chapter 1

**1** <sup>&#123;The Gospel According to&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;Mark&#125;</sup> d <sup>&#123;John the Baptist Prepares the Way for Jesus&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 3:1-12; Luke 3:1-9, 15-17; John 1:19-28)&#125;</sup> d This is <sup>&#123;The Gk. lacks This is&#125;</sup> the beginning of the gospel of Jesus the Messiah, <sup>&#123;Or Christ&#125;</sup> the Son of God. <sup>&#123;Other mss. lack the Son of God&#125;</sup>

**2** As it is written in the prophet Isaiah, "See! I am sending my messenger ahead of you, who will prepare your way. <sup>&#123;Mal 3:1&#125;</sup>

**3** He is a voice calling out in the wilderness: «Prepare the way for the Lord! Make his paths straight!»" <sup>&#123;Isa 40:3&#125;</sup>

**4** John was baptizing in the wilderness, proclaiming a baptism about repentance for the forgiveness of sins.

**5** People from <sup>&#123;The Gk. lacks People from&#125;</sup> the whole Judean countryside and all the people of Jerusalem were flocking to him, being baptized by him while they confessed their sins.

**6** Now John was dressed in camel's hair with <sup>&#123;Lit. and&#125;</sup> a leather belt around his waist. He ate grasshoppers <sup>&#123;Or locust-shaped carob seed pods&#125;</sup> and wild honey.

**7** He kept proclaiming, "The one who is coming after me is stronger than I am, and I am not worthy to bend down and untie his sandal straps.

**8** I baptized you with <sup>&#123;Or in&#125;</sup> water, but it is he who will baptize you with <sup>&#123;Or in&#125;</sup> the Holy Spirit."

**9** <sup>&#123;Jesus is Baptized&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 3:13-17; Luke 3:21-22)&#125;</sup> d In those days Jesus came from Nazareth in Galilee and was baptized by John in the Jordan.

**10** Just as he was coming up out of the water, he saw the heavens split open and the Spirit descending on him like a dove.

**11** Then a voice came from heaven: "You are my Son, whom I love. I am pleased with you!"

**12** <sup>&#123;Jesus is Tempted by Satan&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 4:1-11; Luke 4:1-13)&#125;</sup> d At once the Spirit drove him into the wilderness.

**13** He was in the wilderness for 40 days being tempted by Satan. He was among wild animals, and angels were ministering to him.

**14** <sup>&#123;Jesus Begins His Ministry in Galilee&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 4:12-17; Luke 4:14-15)&#125;</sup> d Now after John had been arrested, Jesus went to Galilee and proclaimed the gospel about the kingdom <sup>&#123;Other mss. lack about the kingdom&#125;</sup> of God.

**15** He said, "The time is now! The kingdom of God is near! Repent, and keep believing the gospel!"

**16** <sup>&#123;Jesus Calls His First Disciples&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 4:18-22; Luke 5:1-11)&#125;</sup> d While Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> was walking beside the Sea of Galilee, he saw Simon and his brother Andrew. They were throwing a net into the sea because they were fishermen.

**17** Jesus told them, "Follow me, and I will make you fishers of people!"

**18** So immediately they left their nets and followed him. /

**19** Going on a little farther he saw James, the son of Zebedee, and his brother John. They were in a boat repairing their nets.

**20** He immediately called them, and they left their father Zebedee in the boat with the hired men and followed him.

**21** <sup>&#123;Jesus Heals a Man with an Unclean Spirit&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Luke 4:31-37)&#125;</sup> d Then they went to Capernaum. As soon as it was the Sabbath, <sup>&#123;Lit. Sabbaths&#125;</sup> Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> went into the synagogue and began to teach.

**22** The people <sup>&#123;Lit. They&#125;</sup> were utterly amazed at his teaching, because he was teaching them like one with authority and not like their scribes.

**23** Suddenly <sup>&#123;Lit. Immediately&#125;</sup> there was a man in their synagogue who had an unclean spirit. He screamed,

**24** "What do you want with us, Jesus of Nazareth? Have you come to destroy us? I know who you are the Holy One of God!"

**25** But Jesus rebuked him, saying, "Be quiet, and come out of him!"

**26** At this, the unclean spirit shook the man, <sup>&#123;Lit. him&#125;</sup> cried out with a loud voice, and came out of him.

**27** All the people were so stunned that they kept saying to each other, "What is this? A new teaching with authority! He tells even the unclean spirits what to do, and they obey him!"

**28** At once his fame began to spread throughout the surrounding region of Galilee.

**29** <sup>&#123;Jesus Heals Many People&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 8:14-17; Luke 4:38-41)&#125;</sup> d After they left the synagogue, they went directly to the house of Simon and Andrew, along with James and John.

**30** Now Simon's mother-in-law was lying in bed, sick with a fever, so they promptly told Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. him&#125;</sup> about her.

**31** He went up to her, took her by the hand, and helped her up. The fever left her, and she began serving them.

**32** When evening came, after the sun had set, people <sup>&#123;Lit. they&#125;</sup> started bringing to him everyone who was sick or possessed by demons.

**33** In fact, the whole city gathered at the door.

**34** He healed many who were sick with various diseases and drove out many demons. However, he wouldn't allow the demons to speak because they knew who he was.

**35** <sup>&#123;Jesus Goes on a Preaching Tour&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Luke 4:42-44)&#125;</sup> d In the morning, while it was still very dark, Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> got up and went to a deserted place and prayed there.

**36** Simon and his companions searched frantically for him.

**37** When they found him, they told him, "Everyone's looking for you."

**38** "Let's go to the neighboring town," he replied, "so I can preach there, too, because that's why I came."

**39** So he went throughout Galilee, preaching in their synagogues and driving out demons.

**40** <sup>&#123;Jesus Cleanses a Leper&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 8:1-4; Luke 5:12-16)&#125;</sup> d Then a leper <sup>&#123;I.e. a man with a serious skin disease&#125;</sup> came to Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. him&#125;</sup> and began pleading with him. He fell on his knees and told him, "If you want to, you can make me clean."

**41** Moved with compassion, Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> reached out his hand, touched him, and told him, "I do want to. Be made clean!"

**42** Instantly the leprosy left him, and he was clean.

**43** Then Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> sternly warned him and encouraged him to go at once,

**44** saying to him, "Be sure that you don't tell anyone. Instead, go and show yourself to the priest, and then offer for your cleansing what Moses commanded as proof to the authorities." <sup>&#123;Lit. to them&#125;</sup>

**45** But when the man <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> left, he began to proclaim it freely. He spread the news so widely that Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> could no longer enter a town openly, but had to stay out in deserted places. Still, people <sup>&#123;Lit. they&#125;</sup> kept coming to him from everywhere.

## Chapter 2

**1** <sup>&#123;Jesus Heals a Paralyzed Man&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 9:1-8; Luke 5:17-26)&#125;</sup> d Several days later, Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> returned to Capernaum and it was reported that he was at home.

**2** Such a large crowd gathered that there wasn't room for them, even in front of the door. Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. He&#125;</sup> was speaking his message to them

**3** when some people <sup>&#123;Lit. they&#125;</sup> came and brought him a paralyzed man being carried by four men.

**4** Since they couldn't bring him to Jesus because of the crowd, they made an opening in the roof over the place where he was. They dug through it and let down the mat on which the paralyzed man was lying.

**5** When Jesus saw their faith, he told the paralyzed man, "Son, your sins are forgiven."

**6** Now some scribes were sitting there, arguing among themselves, <sup>&#123;Lit. in their hearts&#125;</sup>

**7** "Why does this man talk this way? He is blaspheming! Who can forgive sins but God alone?"

**8** At once, Jesus knew in his spirit what they were saying to themselves. "Why are you arguing about such things among yourselves?" <sup>&#123;Lit. in your hearts&#125;</sup> he asked them.

**9** "Which is easier: to say to the paralyzed man, «Your sins are forgiven,'or to say, «Get up, pick up your mat, and walk'?

**10** But I want you to know <sup>&#123;Lit. So that you will know&#125;</sup> that the Son of Man has authority on earth to forgive sins. Then he told the paralyzed man,

**11** "I say to you, get up, pick up your mat, and go home!"

**12** So the man <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> got up, immediately picked up his mat, and went out in front of all of them. As a result, all of the people were amazed and began to glorify God, saying, "We have never seen anything like this!"

**13** <sup>&#123;Jesus Calls Matthew&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 9:9-13; Luke 5:27-32)&#125;</sup> d Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. He&#125;</sup> went out again beside the sea. The whole crowd kept coming to him, and he kept teaching them.

**14** As he was walking along, he saw Levi, the son of Alphaeus, sitting at the tax collector's desk. Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. He&#125;</sup> told him, "Follow me!" So Levi <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> got up and followed him.

**15** Later, he was having dinner at Levi's <sup>&#123;Lit. his&#125;</sup> house. Many tax collectors and sinners were also eating with Jesus and his disciples, because there were many who were following him.

**16** When the scribes and the Pharisees saw him eating with sinners and tax collectors, they asked his disciples, "Why does he eat and drink <sup>&#123;Other mss. lack and drink&#125;</sup> with tax collectors and sinners?"

**17** When Jesus heard that, he told them, "Healthy people don't need a physician, but sick ones do. I did not come to call righteous people, but sinners."

**18** <sup>&#123;A Question about Fasting&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 9:14-17; Luke 5:33-39)&#125;</sup> d Now John's disciples and the Pharisees would fast regularly. Some people <sup>&#123;Lit. They&#125;</sup> came and asked Jesus, <sup>&#123;Lit. him&#125;</sup> "Why do John's disciples and the Pharisees'disciples fast, but your disciples don't fast?"

**19** Jesus replied, "The wedding guests <sup>&#123;Lit. The children of the wedding hall; or The children of the groom&#125;</sup> can't fast while the groom is with them, can they? As long as they have the groom with them, they can't fast.

**20** But the time will come when the groom will be taken away from them, and then they will fast on that day."

**21** <sup>&#123;The Unshrunk Cloth&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 9:16; Luke 5:36)&#125;</sup> d "No one patches an old garment with a piece of unshrunk cloth. If he does, the patch pulls away from it the new from the old and a worse tear is made.

**22** And no one pours new wine into old wineskins. If he does, the wine will make the skins burst, and both the wine and the skins will be ruined. Instead, new wine is poured <sup>&#123;The Gk. lacks is poured&#125;</sup> into fresh wineskins."

**23** <sup>&#123;Jesus is Lord of the Sabbath&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 12:1-8; Luke 6:1-5)&#125;</sup> d Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. He&#125;</sup> happened to be going through the grain fields on a Sabbath. <sup>&#123;Lit. on the Sabbaths&#125;</sup> As they made their way, his disciples began picking the heads of grain.

**24** The Pharisees asked him, "Look! Why are they doing what is not lawful on Sabbath days?" <sup>&#123;Lit. on the Sabbaths&#125;</sup>

**25** He asked them, "Haven't you read what David did when he and his companions were hungry and in need?

**26** How was it that he went into the House of God during the lifetime <sup>&#123;The Gk. lacks the lifetime&#125;</sup> of Abiathar the high priest and ate the Bread of the Presence, which was not lawful for anyone but the priests to eat, and gave some of it to his companions?"

**27** Then he told them, "The Sabbath was made for people, not people for the Sabbath.

**28** Therefore, the Son of Man is Lord even of the Sabbath."

## Chapter 3

**1** <sup>&#123;Jesus Heals a Man with a Paralyzed Hand&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 12:9-14; Luke 6:6-11)&#125;</sup> d Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. He&#125;</sup> went into the synagogue again, and a man with a paralyzed hand was there.

**2** The people <sup>&#123;Lit. They&#125;</sup> watched Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. him&#125;</sup> closely to see whether he would heal him on the Sabbath, <sup>&#123;Lit. Sabbaths&#125;</sup> intending to accuse him of doing something wrong.

**3** He told the man with the paralyzed hand, "Come forward." <sup>&#123;Lit. into the middle&#125;</sup>

**4** Then he asked them, "Is it lawful to do good or to do evil on Sabbath days, <sup>&#123;Lit. on the Sabbaths&#125;</sup> to save a life or to destroy it?" But they remained silent.

**5** Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. He&#125;</sup> looked around at them in anger, deeply hurt because of their hard hearts. Then he told the man, "Hold out your hand." The man <sup>&#123;Lit. He&#125;</sup> held it out, and his hand was restored to health.

**6** Immediately the Pharisees and Herodians <sup>&#123;I.e. Royal party sympathizers&#125;</sup> went out and began to plot how to kill him.

**7** <sup>&#123;Jesus Encounters a Large Crowd&#125;</sup> d So Jesus withdrew with his disciples to the sea. A large crowd from Galilee, Judea,

**8** Jerusalem, Idumea, from across the Jordan, and from the region around Tyre and Sidon followed him. They came to him because they kept hearing about everything he was doing.

**9** Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. He&#125;</sup> told his disciples to have a boat ready for him so that the crowd wouldn't crush him,

**10** because he had healed so many people that everyone who had diseases kept crowding up against him in order to touch him.

**11** Whenever the unclean spirits saw him, they would fall down in front of him and scream, "You are the Son of God!"

**12** But he sternly ordered them again and again not to tell people who he was.

**13** <sup>&#123;Jesus Appoints Twelve Apostles&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 10:1-4; Luke 6:12-16)&#125;</sup> d Then Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> went up on a hillside and called to himself those whom he had decided on, and they approached him.

**14** He appointed the Twelve, <sup>&#123;Or appointed twelve&#125;</sup> whom he called apostles, to accompany him, to be sent out to preach,

**15** and to have the authority to drive out demons.

**16** He appointed the Twelve: <sup>&#123;Other mss. lack He appointed the Twelve&#125;</sup> Simon (whom he named Peter),

**17** Zebedee's sons James and his brother John (whom he named Boanerges, that is, Sons of Thunder),

**18** Andrew, Philip, Bartholomew, Matthew, Thomas, James son of Alphaeus, Thaddeus, <sup>&#123;Other mss. read Lebbaeus&#125;</sup> Simon the Cananaean, <sup>&#123;Cananaean is Aram. for Zealot.&#125;</sup>

**19** and Judas Iscariot, who also betrayed him.

**20** <sup>&#123;Jesus is Accused of Working with Beelzebul&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 12:22-32; Luke 11:14-23; 12:10)&#125;</sup> d Then he went home. Such a large crowd gathered again that Jesus and his disciples <sup>&#123;Lit. so that they&#125;</sup> couldn't even eat.

**21** When his family heard about it, they went to restrain him, because they kept saying, "He's out of his mind!"

**22** The scribes who had come down from Jerusalem kept repeating, "He has Beelzebul,"and, "He drives out demons by the ruler of demons."

**23** So Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> called them together and began to speak to them in parables. "How can Satan drive out Satan?

**24** If a kingdom is divided against itself, that kingdom cannot stand.

**25** And if a household is divided against itself, that household will not stand.

**26** So if Satan rebels against himself and is divided, he cannot stand. Indeed, his end has come.

**27** No one can go into a strong man's house and carry off his possessions without first tying up the strong man. Then he can ransack his house.

**28** I tell all of you  <sup>&#123;The Gk. pronoun you is pl.&#125;</sup> with certainty, people will be forgiven their sins and whatever blasphemies they utter. <sup>&#123;Lit. they blaspheme&#125;</sup>

**29** But whoever blasphemes against the Holy Spirit can never be forgiven, but is guilty of eternal sin."

**30** For they had been saying, "He has an unclean spirit."

**31** <sup>&#123;The True Family of Jesus&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 12:46-50; Luke 8:19-21)&#125;</sup> d Then his mother and his brothers arrived. Milling around outside, they sent for him, continually summoning him.

**32** A crowd was sitting around him. They told him, "Look! Your mother and your brothers <sup>&#123;Other mss. read your brothers and sisters&#125;</sup> are outside asking for you."

**33** He answered them, "Who are my mother and my brothers?"

**34** Then looking at the people sitting around him, he said, "Here are my mother and my brothers!

**35** Whoever does the will of God is my brother and sister and mother."

## Chapter 4

**1** <sup>&#123;The Parable about a Farmer&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 13:1-9; Luke 8:4-18)&#125;</sup> d Then Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> began to teach again beside the sea. Such a large crowd gathered around him that he got into a boat and sat in it, <sup>&#123;Lit. on the sea&#125;</sup> while the entire crowd remained beside the sea on the shore.

**2** He began teaching them many things in parables. While he was teaching them he said,

**3** "Listen! A farmer went out to sow.

**4** As he was sowing, some seeds fell along the path, and birds came and ate them up.

**5** Others fell on stony ground, where they did not have a lot of soil. They sprouted at once because the soil wasn't deep.

**6** But when the sun came up, they were scorched. Since they did not have any roots, they dried up.

**7** Others fell among thorn bushes, and the thorn bushes came up and choked them out, and they did not produce anything.

**8** But others fell on good soil and produced a crop. They grew up, increased in size, and produced 30, 60, or 100 times what was sown."  <sup>&#123;The Gk. lacks what was sown&#125;</sup>

**9** He added, "Let the person who has ears to hear, listen!"

**10** <sup>&#123;The Purpose of the Parables&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 13:10-17; Luke 8:9-10)&#125;</sup> d When he was alone with the Twelve and those around him, they began to ask him about the parables.

**11** He told them, "The secret about the kingdom of God has been given to you. But to those on the outside, everything comes in parables

**12** so that «they may see clearly but not perceive, and they may hear clearly but not understand, otherwise they might turn around and be forgiven.»"  <sup>&#123;Isa 6:9-10&#125;</sup>

**13** <sup>&#123;Jesus Explains the Parable about the Farmer&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 13:18-23; Luke 8:11-15)&#125;</sup> d Then he told them, "You don't understand this parable, so how can you understand any of the parables?

**14** The farmer sows the word.

**15** Some people are like the seeds <sup>&#123;Lit. These are the ones&#125;</sup> along the path, where the word is sown. When they hear it, Satan immediately comes and takes away the word that was sown in them.

**16** Others are like the seeds <sup>&#123;Lit. These are the ones&#125;</sup> sown on the stony ground. When they hear the word, at once they joyfully accept it,

**17** but since they don't have any roots, they last for only a short time. When trouble or persecution comes along because of the word, they immediately fall away.

**18** Still others are like the seeds <sup>&#123;Lit. are those&#125;</sup> sown among the thorn bushes. These are the people who hear the word,

**19** but the worries of life, the deceitful pleasures of wealth, and the desires for other things come in and choke the word so that it can't produce a crop.

**20** Others are like the seeds  <sup>&#123;Lit. are those&#125;</sup> sown on good soil. They hear the word, accept it, and produce crops 30, 60, or 100 times what was sown." <sup>&#123;The Gk. lacks what was sown&#125;</sup>

**21** <sup>&#123;A Light under a Basket&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Luke 8:16-18)&#125;</sup> d Then Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> told them, "A lamp isn't brought indoors to be put under a basket or under a bed, is it? It's to be put on a lamp stand, isn't it?

**22** Nothing is hidden except for the purpose of having it revealed, and nothing is secret except for the purpose of having it come to light.

**23** If anyone has ears to hear, let him listen!

**24** He went on to say to them, "Pay attention to what you're hearing! You will be evaluated by the same standard with which you do your evaluating, and still more will be given to you,

**25** because whoever has something, will have more given to him. But whoever has nothing, even what he has will be taken away."

**26** <sup>&#123;The Parable about a Growing Seed&#125;</sup> d He was also saying, "The kingdom of God is like a man who scatters seeds on the ground.

**27** He sleeps and gets up night and day while the seeds sprout and grow, although he doesn't know how.

**28** The ground produces grain by itself, first the stalk, then the head, then the full grain in the head.

**29** But when the grain is ripe, he immediately starts cutting with his sickle because the harvest time has come."

**30** <sup>&#123;The Parable about a Mustard Seed&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 13:31-32; Luke 13:18-19)&#125;</sup> d He was also saying, "How can we show what the kingdom of God is like, or what parable can we use to describe it?

**31** It is like a mustard seed planted in the ground. Although it is the smallest of <sup>&#123;Or smaller than&#125;</sup> all the seeds on earth,

**32** when it is planted it comes up and becomes larger than all the garden plants. It grows such large branches that the birds in the sky can nest in its shade."

**33** <sup>&#123;Why Jesus Used Parables&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 13:34-35)&#125;</sup> d With many other parables like these, Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> kept speaking his message to them according to their ability to understand.

**34** He did not tell them anything without using <sup>&#123;The Gk. lacks using&#125;</sup> a parable, though he explained everything to his disciples in private.

**35** <sup>&#123;Jesus Calms the Sea&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 8:23-27; Luke 8:22-25)&#125;</sup> d That day, when evening had come, he told them, "Let's cross to the other side."

**36** So they left the crowd and took him away in a boat without making any special preparations. <sup>&#123;Lit. boat just as he was&#125;</sup> Other boats were with him.

**37** A violent windstorm came up, and the waves began breaking into the boat, so that the boat was rapidly <sup>&#123;Lit. already&#125;</sup> becoming swamped.

**38** But Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> was in the back of the boat, asleep on a cushion. So they woke him up and asked him, "Teacher, don't you care that we're going to die?"

**39** Then he got up, rebuked the wind, and told the sea, "Calm down! Be still!" Then the wind stopped blowing, and there was a great calm.

**40** He asked them, "Why are you such cowards? Don't you have any faith yet?"

**41** Overcome with fear, they kept saying to one another, "Who is this man? Even the wind and the sea obey him!"

## Chapter 5

**1** <sup>&#123;Jesus Heals a Demon-Possessed Man&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 8:28-34; Luke 8:26-39)&#125;</sup> d They arrived at the other side of the sea in the territory of the Gerasenes. <sup>&#123;Other mss. read Gergesenes ; still other mss. read Gadarenes&#125;</sup>

**2** Just as Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> stepped out of the boat, a man with an unclean spirit came out of the tombs and met him.

**3** He lived among the tombs, and no one could restrain him any longer, not even with a chain.

**4** He had often been restrained with shackles and chains but had snapped the chains apart and broken the shackles in pieces. No one could tame him.

**5** Night and day he kept screaming among the tombs and on the mountainsides and kept cutting himself with stones.

**6** When he saw Jesus from a distance, he ran and fell down in front of him,

**7** screaming in a loud voice, "What do you want with me, Jesus, Son of the Most High God? I beg you in the name of <sup>&#123;The Gk. lacks the name of&#125;</sup> God never to torment me!"

**8** Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. Because he&#125;</sup> had been saying to him, "Come out of the man, you unclean spirit!"

**9** Then Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> asked him, "What's your name?" He told him, "My name is Legion, <sup>&#123;A Roman legion consisted of about 6, men.&#125;</sup> because there are many of us."

**10** He kept pleading with Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. him&#125;</sup> not to send them out of that region.

**11** Now a large herd of pigs was grazing on a hillside nearby.

**12** So the demons <sup>&#123;Lit. they&#125;</sup> begged him, "Send us among the pigs, so that we can go into them!"

**13** So he let them do this. The unclean spirits came out of the man <sup>&#123;The Gk. lacks of the man&#125;</sup> and went into the pigs, and the herd of about 2, rushed down the cliff into the sea and drowned there. <sup>&#123;Lit. drowned in the sea&#125;</sup>

**14** Now when those who had been taking care of the pigs ran away, they reported what had happened <sup>&#123;Lit. they reported it&#125;</sup> in the city and countryside. So the people <sup>&#123;Lit. they&#125;</sup> went to see what had happened.

**15** When they came to Jesus and saw the man who had been possessed by the legion of demons, sitting there dressed and in his right mind, they were frightened.

**16** The people who had seen it told them what had happened to the demon-possessed man and the pigs.

**17** So they began to beg Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. him&#125;</sup> to leave their territory.

**18** As Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> was getting into the boat, the man who had been demon-possessed kept begging him to let him go with him.

**19** But Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> wouldn't let him. Instead, he told him, "Go home to your family, and tell them how much the Lord has done for you and how merciful he has been to you."

**20** So the man <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> left and began proclaiming in the Decapolis <sup>&#123;Lit. the Ten Cities, a loose federation of ten cities strongly influenced by Greek culture.&#125;</sup> how much Jesus had done for him. And everyone was utterly amazed.

**21** <sup>&#123;Jesus Heals a Woman and Resurrects a Girl&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 9:18-26; Luke 8:40-56)&#125;</sup> d When Jesus again had crossed to the other side in a boat, <sup>&#123;Other mss. lack in a boat&#125;</sup> a large crowd gathered around him by the seashore.

**22** Then a synagogue leader named Jairus arrived. When he saw Jesus, <sup>&#123;Lit. him&#125;</sup> he fell at his feet

**23** and begged him urgently, saying, "My little daughter is dying. Come and lay your hands on her so that she may get well and live."

**24** So Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> went with him. A huge crowd kept following him and jostling him.

**25** Now there was a woman who had been suffering from chronic bleeding for twelve years.

**26** Although she had endured a great deal under the care of many doctors and had spent all of her money, she had not been helped at all but rather grew worse.

**27** Since she had heard about Jesus, she came up behind him in the crowd and touched his robe,

**28** because she had been saying, "If I can just touch his robe, I will get well."

**29** Her bleeding stopped at once, and she felt in her body that she was healed from her illness.

**30** Immediately Jesus became aware that power had gone out of him. So he turned around in the crowd and asked, "Who touched my clothes?"

**31** His disciples asked him, "You see the crowd jostling you, and yet you ask, «Who touched me?» "

**32** But he kept looking around to see the woman who had done this.

**33** So the woman, knowing what had happened to her, came forward fearfully, fell down trembling in front of him, and told him the whole truth.

**34** He told her, "Daughter, your faith has made you well. Go in peace and be healed from your illness."

**35** While he was still speaking, some people <sup>&#123;Lit. they&#125;</sup> came from the synagogue leader's home <sup>&#123;Lit. from the synagogue leader&#125;</sup> and said, "Your daughter is dead. Why bother the Teacher anymore?"

**36** But when Jesus heard <sup>&#123;Other mss. read overheard&#125;</sup> what they said, he told the synagogue leader, "Stop being afraid! Just keep on believing."

**37** Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. He&#125;</sup> allowed no one to go further with him except Peter, James, and John, the brother of James.

**38** When they came to the home of the synagogue leader, Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> saw mass confusion. People <sup>&#123;Lit. They&#125;</sup> were crying and sobbing loudly.

**39** He entered the house <sup>&#123;The Gk. lacks the house&#125;</sup> and asked them, "Why all this confusion and crying? The child isn't dead. She's sleeping."

**40** They laughed and laughed at him. But when he had driven all of them outside, he took the child's father and mother, along with the men who were with him, and went into the room <sup>&#123;The Gk. lacks the room&#125;</sup> where the child was.

**41** He took her by the hand and told her, "Talitha koum," <sup>&#123;Talitha Koum is Heb./Aram. for Little girl, get up!&#125;</sup> which means, "Young lady, I tell you, get up!"

**42** The young lady got up at once and started to walk. She was twelve years old. Instantly they were overcome with astonishment.

**43** But Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> strictly ordered them not to let anyone know about this. He also told them to give her something to eat.

## Chapter 6

**1** <sup>&#123;Jesus is Rejected at Nazareth&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 13:53-58; Luke 4:16-30)&#125;</sup> d Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. He&#125;</sup> left that place and went back to his hometown, <sup>&#123;I.e. Nazareth&#125;</sup> and his disciples followed him.

**2** When the Sabbath came, he began to teach in the synagogue, and many who heard him were utterly amazed. "Where did this man get all these things?" they asked. "What is this wisdom that has been given to him? What great miracles are being done by his hands!

**3** This is the builder, <sup>&#123;Or carpenter&#125;</sup> the son of Mary, and the brother of James, Joseph, Judas, and Simon, isn't it? His sisters are here with us, aren't they?" And they were offended by him.

**4** Jesus was telling them, "A prophet is without honor only in his hometown, among his relatives, and in his own home."

**5** He couldn't perform a miracle there except to lay his hands on a few sick people and heal them.

**6** He was amazed at their unbelief. Then he went around to the villages and continued teaching.

**7** <sup>&#123;Jesus Sends Out the Twelve&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 10:1, 5-15; Luke 9:1-6)&#125;</sup> d He called the Twelve and began to send them out two by two, giving them authority over unclean spirits.

**8** He instructed them to take nothing along on the trip except a walking stick no bread, no traveling bag, nothing in their moneybag.

**9** They could wear sandals but not take along an extra shirt.  <sup>&#123;Lit. along two shirts&#125;</sup>

**10** He told them repeatedly, "Whenever you go into a home, stay there until you leave that place.

**11** If any place will not welcome you and the people <sup>&#123;Lit. they&#125;</sup> refuse to listen to you, when you leave, shake its dust off your feet as a testimony against them."

**12** So they went and preached that people <sup>&#123;Lit. they&#125;</sup> should repent.

**13** They also kept driving out many demons and anointing with oil many who were sick, and healing them.

**14** <sup>&#123;The Death of John the Baptist&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 14:1-12; Luke 9:7-9)&#125;</sup> d King Herod heard about this, because Jesus'<sup>&#123;Lit. his&#125;</sup> name had become well-known. He was <sup>&#123;Other mss. read They were&#125;</sup> saying, "John the Baptist has been raised from the dead! That's why he is able to do these miracles."

**15** Others were saying, "He is Elijah." Still others were saying, "He is a prophet like one of the other <sup>&#123;The Gk. lacks other&#125;</sup> prophets."

**16** But when Herod heard about it, he said, "John, whom I beheaded, has been raised,"

**17** because Herod himself had sent men who arrested <sup>&#123;Lit. sent and arrested&#125;</sup> John, bound him with chains, and put him in prison on account of Herodias, his brother Philip's wife, whom Herod <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> had married.

**18** John had been telling Herod, "It's not lawful for you to have your brother's wife."

**19** So Herodias bore a grudge against John <sup>&#123;Lit. him&#125;</sup> and wanted to kill him. But she couldn't do it

**20** because Herod was afraid of John. He knew that John <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> was a righteous and holy man, and so he protected him. Whenever he listened to John, <sup>&#123;Lit. him&#125;</sup> he did much of what he said. <sup>&#123;Lit. did many things ; other mss. read he became very disturbed&#125;</sup> In fact, he liked listening to him.

**21** An opportunity came during Herod's birthday celebration, when he gave a banquet for his top officials, military officers, and the most important people of Galilee.

**22** When the daughter of Herodias <sup>&#123;Other mss. read his daughter by Herodias&#125;</sup> came in and danced, she pleased Herod and his guests. So the king told the girl, "Ask me for anything you want, and I'll give it to you."

**23** He swore with an oath to her, "I'll give you anything you ask for, up to half of my kingdom."

**24** So she went out and asked her mother, "What should I ask for?" Her mother <sup>&#123;Lit. She&#125;</sup> replied, "The head of John the Baptist."

**25** Immediately the girl <sup>&#123;Lit. she&#125;</sup> hurried back to the king with her request, "I want you to give me right now the head of John the Baptist on a platter."

**26** The king was deeply saddened, yet because of his oaths and his guests he was reluctant to refuse her.

**27** So without delay the king sent a soldier and ordered him to bring John's <sup>&#123;Lit. his&#125;</sup> head. The soldier <sup>&#123;Lit. He&#125;</sup> went and beheaded him in prison.

**28** Then he brought John's <sup>&#123;Lit. his&#125;</sup> head on a platter and gave it to the girl, and the girl gave it to her mother.

**29** When John's <sup>&#123;Lit. his&#125;</sup> disciples heard about this, they came and carried off his body and laid it in a tomb.

**30** <sup>&#123;Jesus Feeds More than Five Thousand People&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 14:13-21; Luke 9:10-17; John 6:1-14)&#125;</sup> d The apostles gathered around Jesus and told him everything they had done and taught.

**31** He told them, "Come away to a deserted place all by yourselves and rest for a while," because so many people were coming and going <sup>&#123;The Gk. lacks and going&#125;</sup> that they didn't even have time to eat.

**32** So they went away in a boat to a deserted place by themselves.

**33** But when many people saw them leave and recognized them, they hurried on foot from all the towns and arrived ahead of them.

**34** When he got out of the boat, <sup>&#123;The Gk. lacks of the boat&#125;</sup> he saw a large crowd. He had compassion for them, because they were like sheep without a shepherd, and he began to teach them many things.

**35** When it was quite late, his disciples came to him and said, "This is a deserted place, and it's already late.

**36** Send the crowds <sup>&#123;Lit. them&#125;</sup> away so that they can go to the neighboring farms and villages and buy themselves something to eat."

**37** But he answered them, "You give them something to eat." They asked him, "Should we go and buy 200 denarii <sup>&#123;The denarius was the usual day's wage for a laborer.&#125;</sup> worth of bread and give it to them to eat?"

**38** He asked them, "How many loaves of bread do you have? Go and see." They found out and told him, "Five loaves <sup>&#123;The Gk. lacks loaves&#125;</sup> and two fish."

**39** Then he ordered them to have all the people sit down in groups on the green grass.

**40** So they sat down in groups of hundreds and fifties.

**41** Taking the five loaves and the two fish, he looked up to heaven and blessed them. Then he broke the loaves in pieces and kept giving them to his disciples to set before the people. <sup>&#123;Lit. before them&#125;</sup> He also divided the two fish among them all.

**42** All of them ate and were filled.

**43** Then the disciples <sup>&#123;Lit. they&#125;</sup> picked up twelve baskets full of leftover bread and fish.

**44** There were 5, men who had eaten the loaves.

**45** <sup>&#123;Jesus Walks on the Sea&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 14:22-33; John 6:16-21)&#125;</sup> d Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. He&#125;</sup> immediately had his disciples get into a boat and cross to Bethsaida ahead of him, while he sent the crowd away.

**46** After saying goodbye to them, he went up on a hillside to pray.

**47** When evening had come, the boat was in the middle of the sea, while he was alone on the land.

**48** He saw that his disciples <sup>&#123;Lit. that they&#125;</sup> were straining at the oars, because the wind was against them. Shortly before dawn <sup>&#123;Lit. In the fourth watch of the night&#125;</sup> he came to them, walking on the sea. He intended to go up right beside them,

**49** but when they saw him walking on the sea, they thought it was a ghost and began to scream.

**50** All of them saw him and were terrified. Immediately he told them, "Have courage! It is I. Stop being afraid!"

**51** Then he got into the boat with them, and the wind stopped blowing. The disciples <sup>&#123;Lit. They&#125;</sup> were utterly astounded,

**52** because they didn't understand the significance of the loaves. Instead, their hearts were hardened.

**53** <sup>&#123;Jesus Heals the Sick in Gennesaret&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 14:34-36)&#125;</sup> d When they had crossed over, they came ashore at Gennesaret and anchored the boat.

**54** As soon as they got out of the boat, the people recognized Jesus. <sup>&#123;Lit. him&#125;</sup>

**55** They ran all over the countryside and began carrying the sick on their mats to any place where they heard he was.

**56** Wherever he went, whether into villages, towns, or farms, people <sup>&#123;Lit. they&#125;</sup> would place their sick in the marketplaces and beg him to let them touch even the tassel of his garment, and everyone who touched it was healed.

## Chapter 7

**1** <sup>&#123;Jesus Challenges the Tradition of the Elders&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 15:1-20)&#125;</sup> d The Pharisees and some of the scribes who had come from Jerusalem gathered around Jesus. <sup>&#123;Lit. him&#125;</sup>

**2** They noticed that some of his disciples were eating <sup>&#123;Lit. eating bread&#125;</sup> with unclean hands, that is, without washing them.

**3** (The Pharisees and indeed all the Jewish people don't eat unless they wash their hands properly, <sup>&#123;Lit. with a fist&#125;</sup> following the tradition of their elders.

**4** They don't eat anything from the marketplace unless they dip it in water. They also observe many other traditions, such as the proper washing of washing cups, jars, brass pots, and dinner tables.) <sup>&#123;Other mss. lack and dinner tables&#125;</sup>

**5** So the Pharisees and the scribes asked Jesus, <sup>&#123;Lit. him&#125;</sup> "Why don't your disciples live according to the tradition of the elders? Instead, they eat <sup>&#123;Lit. eat bread&#125;</sup> with unclean hands."

**6** He told them, "Isaiah was right when he prophesied about you hypocrites. As it is written, «These people honor me with their lips, but their hearts are far from me.

**7** Their worship of me is worthless, because they teach human rules as doctrines.' <sup>&#123;Isa 29:13&#125;</sup>

**8** You abandon the commandment of God and hold to human tradition."

**9** Then he told them, "You have such a fine way of rejecting the commandment of God in order to keep your own tradition!

**10** Because Moses said, «Honor your father and your mother,' <sup>&#123;Exod 20:12; Deut 5:16&#125;</sup> and, «Whoever curses his father or mother must certainly be put to death.'<sup>&#123;Exod 21:17; Lev 20:9&#125;</sup>

**11** But you say, «If anyone tells his father or mother, "Whatever support you might have received from me is Corban,"' (that is, an offering to God)

**12** «you no longer let him do anything for his father or mother.'

**13** You are destroying the word of God through your tradition that you have handed down. And you do many other things like that."

**14** Then he called to the crowd again and told them, "Listen to me, all of you, and understand!

**15** Nothing that goes into a person from the outside can make him unclean. It's what comes out of a person that makes a person unclean.

**16** If anyone has ears to hear, let him listen!"  <sup>&#123;Other mss. lack this verse.&#125;</sup>

**17** When he had left the crowd and gone home, his disciples began asking him about the parable.

**18** He asked them, "Are you so ignorant? Don't you know that nothing that goes into a person from the outside can make him unclean?

**19** Because it doesn't go into his heart but into his stomach, and then into the sewer, <sup>&#123;Or drain&#125;</sup> thereby expelling <sup>&#123;7:19Or cleansing from ; the Gk. lacks thereby&#125;</sup> all foods."

**20** Then he continued, "It's what comes out of a person that makes a person unclean,

**21** because it's from within, from the human heart, that evil thoughts come, as well as sexual immorality, stealing, murder,

**22** adultery, greed, wickedness, cheating, shameless lust, envy, slander, <sup>&#123;Or blasphemy&#125;</sup> arrogance, and foolishness.

**23** All these things come from inside and make a person unclean."

**24** <sup>&#123;A Canaanite Woman's Faith&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 15:21-28)&#125;</sup> d Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. He&#125;</sup> left that place and went to the territory of Tyre and Sidon. <sup>&#123;Other mss. lack and Sidon&#125;</sup> He went into a house, not wanting anyone to know he was there. However, it couldn't be kept a secret.

**25** In fact, a woman whose little daughter had an unclean spirit immediately heard about him and came and fell down at his feet.

**26** Now the woman happened to be a Greek, born in Phoenicia in Syria. She kept asking him to drive the demon out of her daughter.

**27** But he kept telling her, "First let the children be filled. It is not right to take the children's bread and throw it to the puppies."

**28** But she answered him, "Yes, <sup>&#123;Other mss. lack Yes&#125;</sup> Lord. Yet even the puppies under the table eat some of the children's crumbs."

**29** Then he told her, "Because you have said this, go! The demon has left your daughter."

**30** So she went home and found her child lying in bed, and the demon was gone.

**31** <sup>&#123;Jesus Heals a Deaf Man with a Speech Impediment&#125;</sup> d Then Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> left the territory of Tyre and passed through Sidon towards the Sea of Galilee, in the territory of the Decapolis. <sup>&#123;Lit. the Ten Cities, a loose federation of ten cities strongly influenced by Greek culture&#125;</sup>

**32** Some people <sup>&#123;Lit. They&#125;</sup> brought him a deaf man who also had a speech impediment. They begged him to lay his hand on him.

**33** Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. He&#125;</sup> took him away from the crowd to be alone with him. Putting his fingers into the man's <sup>&#123;Lit. his&#125;</sup> ears, he touched the man's <sup>&#123;Lit. his&#125;</sup> tongue with saliva.

**34** Then he looked up to heaven, sighed, and told him, "Ephphatha," <sup>&#123;Ephphatha is Heb./Aram. for Be opened!&#125;</sup> that is, "Be opened!"

**35** The man's <sup>&#123;Lit. his&#125;</sup> hearing and speech were restored at once, and he began to talk normally.

**36** Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. He&#125;</sup> ordered the people <sup>&#123;Lit. them&#125;</sup> not to tell anyone, but the more he kept ordering them, the more they kept spreading the news.

**37** Amazed beyond measure, they kept on saying, "He does everything well! He even makes deaf people hear and mute people talk!"

## Chapter 8

**1** <sup>&#123;Jesus Feeds More than Four Thousand People&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 15:32-39)&#125;</sup> d At that time, after a large crowd again had gathered together with nothing to eat, Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> called his disciples and told them,

**2** "I have compassion for the crowd because they have already been with me for three days and have nothing to eat.

**3** If I send them away to their homes hungry, they will faint on the road. Some of them have come a long distance."

**4** His disciples answered him, "Where could anyone get enough bread to feed these people out here in the wilderness?"

**5** He asked them, "How many loaves of bread do you have?" "Seven," they said.

**6** So he ordered the crowd to sit down on the ground. Then he took the seven loaves and gave thanks. He broke them in pieces and kept giving them to his disciples to distribute. So they served them to the crowd.

**7** They also had a few small fish. He blessed them and said that the fish <sup>&#123;Lit. these&#125;</sup> should also be distributed.

**8** The people <sup>&#123;Lit. They&#125;</sup> ate and were filled. Then the disciples <sup>&#123;Lit. they&#125;</sup> picked up the leftover pieces seven large baskets full.

**9** Now about 4, men were there. Then he sent them on their way.

**10** Immediately he got into a boat with his disciples and went to the region of Dalmanutha. <sup>&#123;Other mss. read Mageda ; still other mss. read Magdala&#125;</sup>

**11** <sup>&#123;Interpreting the Time&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 16:1-4; Luke 12:54-56)&#125;</sup> d The Pharisees arrived and began arguing with Jesus. <sup>&#123;Lit. him&#125;</sup> They tested him by demanding from him a sign from heaven.

**12** He sighed deeply in his spirit and remarked, "Why do those living today <sup>&#123;Lit. Why does this generation&#125;</sup> demand a sign? I tell all of you <sup>&#123;The Gk. pronoun you is pl.&#125;</sup> with certainty, no sign will be given to this generation."

**13** Leaving them, he got into a boat again and crossed to the other side.

**14** <sup>&#123;The Yeast of the Pharisees and Sadducees&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 16:5-12)&#125;</sup> d Now the disciples <sup>&#123;Lit. they&#125;</sup> had forgotten to take any bread along, but they had one loaf with them in the boat.

**15** Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. He&#125;</sup> had been warning them, "Watch out! Beware of the yeast of the Pharisees and the yeast of Herod!" <sup>&#123;Other mss. read of the Herodians&#125;</sup>

**16** So they were discussing with one another the fact that they didn't have any bread.

**17** Knowing this, Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> asked them, "Why are you discussing the fact that you don't have any bread? Don't you understand or perceive yet? Are your hearts hardened?

**18** Do you have eyes but fail to see? Do you have ears but fail to hear? <sup>&#123;Jer 5:21&#125;</sup> Don't you remember?

**19** When I broke the five loaves for the 5,, how many baskets did you fill with leftover pieces?" They told him, "Twelve."

**20** "When I broke <sup>&#123;The Gk. lacks I broke&#125;</sup> the seven loaves <sup>&#123;The Gk. lacks loaves&#125;</sup> for the 4,, how many large baskets did you fill with the leftover pieces?" They told him, "Seven."

**21** Then he asked them, "Don't you understand yet?"

**22** <sup>&#123;Jesus Heals a Blind Man in Bethsaida&#125;</sup> d As they came to Bethsaida, some people <sup>&#123;Lit. they&#125;</sup> brought a blind man to Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. him&#125;</sup> and begged him to touch him. <sup>&#123;The Gk. lacks him&#125;</sup>

**23** Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. Then he&#125;</sup> took the blind man by the hand and led him out of the village. He spit into his eyes, placed his hands on him, and asked him, "Do you see anything?"

**24** The man <sup>&#123;Lit. He&#125;</sup> looked up and said, "I see people, but they look like trees walking around."

**25** Then Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> placed his hands on the man's <sup>&#123;Lit. his&#125;</sup> eyes again, and he saw clearly. His sight was restored, and he saw everything perfectly even from a distance.

**26** Then Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> sent him home, saying, "Don't go into the village or tell anyone in the village." <sup>&#123;Other mss. lack or tell anyone in the village&#125;</sup>

**27** <sup>&#123;Peter Declares His Faith in Jesus&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 16:13-20; Luke 9:18-21)&#125;</sup> d Then Jesus and his disciples set out for the villages around Caesarea Philippi. On the way he was asking his disciples, "Who do people say I am?"

**28** They answered him, "Some say <sup>&#123;The Gk. lacks Some say&#125;</sup> John the Baptist, others Elijah, and still others one of the prophets."

**29** Then he began to ask them, "But who do you say I am?" Peter answered him, "You are the Messiah!" &#123;Or Christ&#125;

**30** Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. He&#125;</sup> sternly ordered them not to tell anyone about him.

**31** <sup>&#123;Jesus Predicts His Death and Resurrection&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 16:21-28; Luke 9:21-27)&#125;</sup> d Then he began to teach them that the Son of Man would have to suffer a great deal and be rejected by the elders, the high priests, and the scribes. Then he would be killed, but after three days he would rise again.

**32** He was speaking about this matter quite openly. Peter took him aside and began to rebuke him.

**33** But turning and looking at his disciples, Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> rebuked Peter, saying, "Get behind me, Satan, because you're not thinking God's thoughts but human thoughts!"

**34** Then Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> called the crowd to himself along with his disciples and told them, "If anyone wants to follow me, he must deny himself, pick up his cross, and follow me continually,

**35** because whoever wants to save his life will lose it, but whoever loses his life for my sake and for the gospel will save it.

**36** What profit will a person have if he gains the whole world and forfeits his life?

**37** Indeed, what can a person give in exchange for his life?

**38** If anyone is ashamed of me and my words in this adulterous and sinful generation, the Son of Man will be ashamed of him when he comes with the holy angels in his Father's glory."

## Chapter 9

**1** Then he told them, "I tell all of you <sup>&#123;The Gk. pronoun you is pl.&#125;</sup> with certainty, some people standing here will not experience <sup>&#123;Lit. taste&#125;</sup> death until they see the kingdom of God arrive with power."

**2** <sup>&#123;Jesus'Appearance is Changed&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 17:1-13; Luke 9:28-36)&#125;</sup> d Six days later, Jesus took Peter, James, and John and led them up a high mountain to be alone with him. His appearance was changed in front of them,

**3** and his clothes became dazzling white, whiter than anyone <sup>&#123;Lit. anyone who cleans&#125;</sup> on earth could bleach them.

**4** Then Elijah appeared to them, accompanied by Moses, and they were talking with Jesus.

**5** Then Peter told Jesus, "Rabbi, <sup>&#123;Rabbi is Heb. for Master and/or Teacher&#125;</sup> it's good that we're here! Let's set up three shelters <sup>&#123;Or tents&#125;</sup> one for you, one for Moses, and one for Elijah."

**6** (Peter <sup>&#123;Lit. He&#125;</sup> didn't know how to respond, because they were terrified.)

**7** Then a cloud appeared and overshadowed them. A voice came out of the cloud and said, <sup>&#123;The Gk. lacks and said&#125;</sup> "This is my Son, whom I love. Keep on listening to him!"

**8** Suddenly, as they looked around, they saw no one with them but Jesus alone.

**9** On their way down the mountain, Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> ordered them not to tell anyone what they had seen until the Son of Man had risen from the dead.

**10** They kept the matter to themselves but argued about what "rising from the dead" meant.

**11** So they asked him, "Don't the scribes say that Elijah must come first?"

**12** He told them, "Elijah is indeed coming first and will restore all things. Why, then, is it written that the Son of Man must suffer a great deal and be treated shamefully?

**13** But I tell you that Elijah has come, yet people <sup>&#123;Lit. they&#125;</sup> treated him just as they pleased, as it is written about him."

**14** <sup>&#123;Jesus Heals a Boy with a Demon&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 17:14-20)&#125;</sup> d As they approached the other <sup>&#123;The Gk. lacks other&#125;</sup> disciples, they saw a large crowd around them and some scribes arguing with them.

**15** The whole crowd was very surprised to see Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. him&#125;</sup> and ran to welcome him.

**16** He asked the scribes, <sup>&#123;Lit. them&#125;</sup> "What are you arguing about with them?"

**17** A man in the crowd answered him, "Teacher, I brought my son to you. He has a spirit that won't let him talk.

**18** Whenever it brings on a seizure, it throws him to the ground. Then he foams at the mouth, grinds his teeth, and becomes stiff. So I asked your disciples to drive the spirit <sup>&#123;Lit. it&#125;</sup> out, but they didn't have the power."

**19** Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. He&#125;</sup> told them, "You unbelieving generation! How long must I be with you? How long must I put up with you? Bring him to me!"

**20** So they brought the boy <sup>&#123;Lit. him&#125;</sup> to him. When the spirit saw Jesus, <sup>&#123;Lit. him&#125;</sup> it immediately threw the boy <sup>&#123;Lit. him&#125;</sup> into convulsions. He fell on the ground and kept rolling around and foaming at the mouth.

**21** Then Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> asked his father, "How long has this been happening to him?" He said, "Since he was a child.

**22** The spirit <sup>&#123;Lit. It&#125;</sup> has often thrown him into fire and into water to destroy him. But if you are able to do anything, have pity on us and help us!"

**23** Jesus told him, «If you are able? 'Everything is possible for the person who believes!"

**24** With tears flowing, <sup>&#123;Other mss. lack With tears flowing&#125;</sup> the child's father at once cried out, "I do believe! Help my unbelief!"

**25** When Jesus saw that a crowd was running to the scene, he rebuked the unclean spirit, saying to it, "You spirit that won't let him talk or hear I command you to come out of him and never enter him again!"

**26** The spirit <sup>&#123;Lit. It&#125;</sup> screamed, shook the child <sup>&#123;The Gk. lacks the child&#125;</sup> violently, and came out. The boy was like a corpse, and many said that he was dead.

**27** But Jesus took his hand and helped him up, and he stood up.

**28** When Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> came home, his disciples asked him privately, "Why couldn't we drive the spirit <sup>&#123;Lit. drive it&#125;</sup> out?"

**29** He told them, "This kind can come out only by prayer and fasting." <sup>&#123;Other mss. lack and fasting&#125;</sup>

**30** <sup>&#123;Jesus Again Predicts His Death and Resurrection&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 17:22-23; Luke 9:43-45)&#125;</sup> d Then they left that place and passed through Galilee. Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. He&#125;</sup> didn't want anyone to find out about it,

**31** because he was teaching his disciples, "The Son of Man will be betrayed into human hands. They will kill him, but after being dead for three days he will be raised."

**32** They didn't understand what this statement meant, and they were afraid to ask him.

**33** <sup>&#123;True Greatness&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 18:1-5; Luke 9:46-48)&#125;</sup> d Then they came to Capernaum. While Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> was at home, he asked the disciples, <sup>&#123;Lit. them&#125;</sup> "What were you arguing about on the road?"

**34** But they kept silent, because they had argued on the road with one another about who was the greatest.

**35** So he sat down, called the Twelve, and told them, "If anyone wants to be first he must be last of all and servant of all."

**36** Then he took a little child and had him stand among them. He took him in his arms and told them,

**37** "Whoever welcomes a child like this in my name welcomes me, and whoever welcomes me welcomes not me but the one who sent me."

**38** <sup>&#123;The True Follower of Jesus&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Luke 9:49-50)&#125;</sup> d John told Jesus, <sup>&#123;Lit. him&#125;</sup> "Teacher, we saw someone driving out demons in your name. We tried to stop him, because he wasn't a follower like us."

**39** But Jesus said, "Don't stop him, because no one who works a miracle in my name can slander me soon afterwards.

**40** Whoever is not against us is for us.

**41** I tell all of you <sup>&#123;The Gk. pronoun you is pl.&#125;</sup> with certainty, whoever gives you a cup of water to drink because you belong to the Messiah <sup>&#123;Or Christ&#125;</sup> will never lose his reward."

**42** <sup>&#123;Causing Others to Sin&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 18:6-9; Luke 17:1-2)&#125;</sup> d "If anyone causes one of these little ones who believe in me to sin, it would be better for him if a large millstone were hung around his neck and he were thrown into the sea.

**43** So if your hand causes you to sin, cut it off. It is better for you to enter life injured than to have two hands and go to hell, <sup>&#123;Lit. Gehenna ; a Gk. transliteration of the Heb. for Valley of Hinnom&#125;</sup> to the fire that cannot be put out.

**44** In that place, worms never die, and the fire is never put out.  <sup>&#123;Other mss. lack this verse&#125;</sup>

**45** And if your foot causes you to sin, cut it off. It is better for you to enter life crippled than to have two feet and be thrown into hell.  <sup>&#123;Lit. Gehenna ; a Gk. transliteration of the Heb. for Valley of Hinnom&#125;</sup>

**46** In that place, worms never die, and the fire is never put out.  <sup>&#123;Other mss. lack this verse&#125;</sup>

**47** And if your eye causes you to sin, tear it out. It is better for you to enter the kingdom of God with one eye than to have two eyes and be thrown into hell.  <sup>&#123;Lit. Gehenna ; a Gk. transliteration of the Heb. for Valley of Hinnom&#125;</sup>

**48** In that place, worms never die, and the fire is never put out.

**49** Because everyone will be salted with fire, and every sacrifice will be salted with salt.  <sup>&#123;Other mss. lack and every sacrifice will be salted with salt&#125;</sup>

**50** Salt is good. But if salt loses its taste, how can you restore its flavor? Keep on having salt among yourselves, and live in peace with one another."

## Chapter 10

**1** <sup>&#123;Teaching about Divorce&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 19:1-12)&#125;</sup> d Then Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> left that place and went into the territory of Judea on the other side <sup>&#123;I.e. the east side&#125;</sup> of the Jordan. Crowds gathered around him as usual, and he began to teach them again as was his custom.

**2** Some Pharisees came to test him. They asked, "Is it lawful for a man to divorce his wife?"

**3** He answered them, "What did Moses command you?"

**4** They said, "Moses allowed a man to write a certificate of divorce and to divorce her." <sup>&#123;Deut 24:1, 3&#125;</sup>

**5** But Jesus told them, "It was because of your hardness of heart that he wrote this command for you.

**6** But from the beginning of creation, «God <sup>&#123;Other mss. read He&#125;</sup> made them male and female.' <sup>&#123;Gen 1:27; 5:2&#125;</sup>

**7** "That is why «a man will leave his father and mother and be united with his wife,

**8** and the two will become one flesh.'<sup>&#123;Gen 2:24&#125;</sup> So they are no longer two, but one flesh.

**9** Therefore, what God has joined together, man must never separate."

**10** Back in the house, the disciples asked him about this again.

**11** So he told them, "Whoever divorces his wife and marries another woman commits adultery against her.

**12** And if a woman <sup>&#123;Lit. she&#125;</sup> divorces her husband and marries another man, she commits adultery."

**13** <sup>&#123;Jesus Blesses the Little Children&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 19:13-15; Luke 18:15-17)&#125;</sup> d Some people <sup>&#123;Lit. They&#125;</sup> were bringing little children to Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. him&#125;</sup> to have him touch them. But the disciples rebuked those who brought <sup>&#123;The Gk. lacks those who brought&#125;</sup> them.

**14** When Jesus saw this, he became furious and told them, "Let the little children come to me, and stop keeping them away, because the kingdom of God belongs to people like these.

**15** I tell all of you <sup>&#123;The Gk. pronoun you is pl.&#125;</sup> with certainty, whoever doesn't receive the kingdom of God as a little child will never enter it."

**16** Then after he had hugged the children, <sup>&#123;Lit. hugged them&#125;</sup> he tenderly blessed them as he laid his hands on them.

**17** <sup>&#123;A Rich Man Comes to Jesus&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 19:16-30; Luke 18:18-30)&#125;</sup> d As Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> was setting out again, <sup>&#123;Lit. out on a journey&#125;</sup> a man ran up to him, knelt down in front of him, and asked him, "Good Teacher, what must I do to inherit eternal life?"

**18** "Why do you call me good?" Jesus asked him. "Nobody is good except for one God.

**19** You know the commandments: «Never murder.' <sup>&#123;Exod 20:13; Deut 5:17&#125;</sup> «Never commit adultery.'<sup>&#123;Exod 20:14; Deut 5:18&#125;</sup> «Never steal.'<sup>&#123;Exod 20:15; Deut 5:19&#125;</sup> «Never give false testimony.'<sup>&#123;Exod 20:16; Deut 5:20&#125;</sup> «Never cheat.'«Honor your father and mother.»" <sup>&#123;Exod 20:12; Deut 5:16&#125;</sup>

**20** The man <sup>&#123;Lit. He&#125;</sup> replied to him, "Teacher, I have obeyed all of these since I was a young man."

**21** Jesus looked at him and loved him. Then he told him, "You're missing one thing. Go and sell everything you own, give the money <sup>&#123;The Gk. lacks the money&#125;</sup> to the destitute, and you will have treasure in heaven. Then come back and follow me."

**22** Shocked at this statement, the man <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> went away sad, because he had many possessions.

**23** <sup>&#123;Salvation and Reward&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 19:23-26; Luke 18:24-30)&#125;</sup> d Then Jesus looked around and told his disciples, "How hard it will be for those who are wealthy to enter the kingdom of God!"

**24** The disciples were startled by these words, but Jesus told them again, "Children, how hard it is for those who trust in their wealth <sup>&#123;Other mss. lack for those who trust in their wealth&#125;</sup> to get into the kingdom of God!

**25** It is easier for a camel to squeeze through the eye of a needle than for a rich person to get into the kingdom of God."

**26** The disciples <sup>&#123;Lit. They&#125;</sup> were utterly amazed and asked one another, <sup>&#123;Other mss. read to him&#125;</sup> "Then who can be saved?"

**27** Jesus looked at them intently and said, "For humans it is impossible, but not for God. All things are possible for God."

**28** Then Peter began to say to him, "See, we have left everything and followed you."

**29** Jesus said, "I tell all of you <sup>&#123;The Gk. pronoun you is pl.&#125;</sup> with certainty, there is no one who has left his home, brothers, sisters, mother, father, children, or fields because of me and the gospel

**30** who will not receive a hundred times as much here in this world homes, brothers, sisters, mothers, children, and fields, along with persecution as well as eternal life in the age to come.

**31** But many who are first will be last, and the last will be first."

**32** Now Jesus and his disciples <sup>&#123;Lit. They&#125;</sup> had been on the road going up to Jerusalem, with Jesus walking ahead of them. They were astonished, and the others who followed were afraid. <sup>&#123;Jesus Predicts His Death and Resurrection a Third Time&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 20:17-19; Luke 18:31-34)&#125;</sup> d Once again, Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> took the Twelve aside and began to tell them what was going to happen to him.

**33** "See, we are going up to Jerusalem. The Son of Man will be handed over to the high priests and the scribes, and they will condemn him to death. Then they will hand him over to the unbelievers,  <sup>&#123;Lit. gentiles ; i.e. unbelieving non-Jews&#125;</sup>

**34** and they will make fun of him, spit on him, whip him, and kill him. But after three days he will be raised."

**35** <sup>&#123;The Request of James and John&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 20:20-28)&#125;</sup> d James and John, the sons of Zebedee, went to Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. him&#125;</sup> and told him, "Teacher, we want you to do for us whatever we ask you."

**36** He asked them, "What do you want me to do for you?"

**37** They asked him, "Let us sit in your glory, one on your right and one on your left."

**38** But Jesus told them, "You don't realize what you're asking. Can you drink from the cup that I'm going to drink from or be baptized with the baptism with which I'm going to be baptized?"

**39** They told him, "We can." Jesus told them, "You will drink from the cup that I'm going to drink and be baptized with the baptism with which I'm going to be baptized.

**40** But it's not up to me to grant you a seat at my right or my left. Those positions have already been prepared for others."

**41** When the ten other disciples <sup>&#123;The Gk. lacks other disciples&#125;</sup> heard this, they began to be furious with James and John.

**42** Jesus called his disciples <sup>&#123;Lit. called them&#125;</sup> and told them, "You know that those who are recognized as rulers among the unbelievers <sup>&#123;Lit. gentiles; i.e. unbelieving non-Jews&#125;</sup> lord it over them, and their superiors act like tyrants over them.

**43** That's not the way it should be among you. Instead, whoever wants to become great among you must be your servant,

**44** and whoever wants to be first among you must be a slave to everyone,

**45** because even the Son of Man did not come to be served, but to serve and to give his life as a ransom for many people."

**46** <sup>&#123;Jesus Heals Blind Bartimaeus&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 20:29-34; Luke 18:35-43)&#125;</sup> d Then they came to Jericho. As Jesus, <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> his disciples, and a large crowd were leaving Jericho, a blind beggar named Bartimaeus (the son of Timaeus) was sitting by the road.

**47** When he heard that Jesus of Nazareth was there, he began to shout, "Jesus, Son of David, have mercy on me!"

**48** Many people sternly told him to be quiet, but he started shouting even louder, "Son of David, have mercy on me!"

**49** So Jesus stopped and said, "Call him!" So they called the blind man and told him, "Have courage! Get up. He's calling you."

**50** He threw off his coat, jumped up, and went to Jesus.

**51** Then Jesus asked him, "What do you want me to do for you?" The blind man told him, "Rabbouni, <sup>&#123;Rabbouni is Heb. for My Master and/or Teacher&#125;</sup> I want to see again."

**52** Jesus told him, "Go. Your faith has made you well." At once the man <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> could see again, and he began to follow Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. him&#125;</sup> down the road.

## Chapter 11

**1** <sup>&#123;The King Enters Jerusalem&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 21:1-11; Luke 19:28-40; John 12:12-19)&#125;</sup> d When they came near Jerusalem, at Bethphage and Bethany, near the Mount of Olives, Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> sent two of his disciples on ahead

**2** and told them, "Go into the village ahead of you. As soon as you go into it, you will find a colt tied up that no one has ever ridden. Untie it, and bring it along.

**3** If anyone asks you, «Why are you doing this?,'say, «The Lord needs it,'and he will send it back here at once."

**4** So they went and found the colt outside in the street tied up next to a doorway. While they were untying it,

**5** some men standing there asked them, "What are you doing untying that colt?"

**6** The disciples <sup>&#123;Lit. They&#125;</sup> told them what Jesus had said, and the men <sup>&#123;Lit. they&#125;</sup> let them go.

**7** They brought the colt to Jesus and threw their coats upon it, and he sat on it.

**8** Many people spread their coats on the road, while others spread leafy branches that they had cut in the fields.

**9** Those who went ahead and those who followed him were shouting, "Hosanna! <sup>&#123;Hosanna is Heb. for Please save or Praise.&#125;</sup> How blessed is the one who comes in the name of the Lord!

**10** How blessed is the coming kingdom <sup>&#123;Ps 148:1&#125;</sup> of our ancestor David! Hosanna in the highest heaven!" <sup>&#123;Ps 118:25-26; Ps 148:1&#125;</sup>

**11** Then Jesus went into Jerusalem and into the Temple and looked around at everything. Since it was already late, he went out with the Twelve to Bethany.

**12** <sup>&#123;Jesus Curses a Fig Tree&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 21:18-19)&#125;</sup> d The next day, as they were leaving Bethany, Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> became hungry.

**13** Seeing in the distance a fig tree covered with leaves, he went to see if he could find anything on it. When he came to it, he found nothing except leaves because it wasn't the season for figs.

**14** So he told it, "May no one ever eat fruit from you again!" Now his disciples were listening to this.

**15** <sup>&#123;Confrontation in the Temple over Money&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 21:12-17; Luke 19:45-48; John 2:13-22)&#125;</sup> d When they came to Jerusalem, he went into the Temple and began to throw out those who were selling and those who were buying in the Temple. He overturned the moneychangers'tables and the chairs of those who sold doves.

**16** He wouldn't even let anyone carry a vessel through the Temple.

**17** Then he began to teach them: "It is written, is it not, «My house is to be called a house of prayer for all nations'? <sup>&#123;Isa 56:7; Jer 7:11&#125;</sup> But you have turned it into a hideout <sup>&#123;Lit. cave&#125;</sup> for bandits!"

**18** When the high priests and elders heard this, they began to look for a way to kill him, because they were afraid of him, since the whole crowd was amazed at his teaching.

**19** When evening came, Jesus and his disciples <sup>&#123;Lit. came, they&#125;</sup> would leave the city.

**20** <sup>&#123;The Lesson from the Dried Fig Tree&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 21:20-22)&#125;</sup> d While they were walking along early the next morning, they saw the fig tree dried up to its roots.

**21** Remembering what Jesus had said, <sup>&#123;The Gk. lacks what Jesus had said&#125;</sup> Peter pointed out to him, "Rabbi, <sup>&#123;Rabbi is Heb. for Master and/or Teacher&#125;</sup> look! The fig tree you cursed has dried up!"

**22** Jesus told his disciples, <sup>&#123;Lit. to them&#125;</sup> "Have faith in God!

**23** I tell all of you <sup>&#123;The Gk. pronoun you is pl.&#125;</sup> with certainty, if anyone says to this mountain, «Be lifted up and thrown into the sea,'if he doesn't doubt in his heart but believes that what he says will happen, it will be done for him.

**24** That is why I tell you, whatever you ask for in prayer, believe that you have received <sup>&#123;Other mss. read are receiving ; still other mss. read will receive&#125;</sup> it and it will be yours.

**25** "Whenever you stand up to pray, forgive whatever you have against anyone, so that your Father in heaven will forgive your sins.

**26** But if you do not forgive, your Father in heaven will not forgive your sins." <sup>&#123;Other mss. lack this verse&#125;</sup>

**27** <sup>&#123;Jesus'Authority is Challenged&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 21:23-27; Luke 20:1-8)&#125;</sup> d Then they went into Jerusalem again. While Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> was walking in the Temple, the high priests, the scribes, and the elders came to him

**28** and asked him, "By what authority are you doing these things, and who gave you this authority to do them?"

**29** Jesus told them, "I will ask you one question. <sup>&#123;Lit. one word&#125;</sup> Answer me, and then I will tell you by what authority I am doing these things.

**30** Was John's authority to baptize <sup>&#123;Lit. John's baptism&#125;</sup> from heaven or from humans? Answer me."

**31** They began discussing this among themselves. "If we say, «From heaven,'he will say, «Then why didn't you believe him? '

**32** But if we say, «From humans»?" They were afraid of the crowd, because everyone really thought John was a prophet.

**33** So they answered Jesus, "We don't know." Then Jesus told them, "Then I won't tell you by what authority I am doing these things."

## Chapter 12

**1** <sup>&#123;The Parable about the Tenant Farmers&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 21:33-46; Luke 20:9-19)&#125;</sup> d Then Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> began to speak to them in parables. "A man planted a vineyard. He put a wall around it, dug a pit for the wine press, and built a watchtower. Then he leased it to tenant farmers and went abroad.

**2** At the right time, he sent a servant to the farmers to collect from them a share of the produce from the vineyard.

**3** But the farmers <sup>&#123;Lit. they&#125;</sup> grabbed the servant, <sup>&#123;Lit. him&#125;</sup> beat him, and sent him back empty-handed.

**4** Again, the man <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> sent another servant to them. They beat the servant <sup>&#123;Lit. him&#125;</sup> over the head and treated him shamefully.

**5** Then the man <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> sent another, and that one they killed. So it was with many other servants. <sup>&#123;Lit. with many others&#125;</sup> Some of these they beat, and others they killed.

**6** He still had one more person to send, <sup>&#123;The Gk. lacks more person to send&#125;</sup> a son whom he loved. Finally, he sent him to them, saying, «They will respect my son.»

**7** But those farmers told one another, «This is the heir. Come on, let's kill him, and the inheritance will be ours!'

**8** So they grabbed him, killed him, and threw him out of the vineyard.

**9** "Now what will the owner of the vineyard do? He will come, execute the farmers, and give the vineyard to others.

**10** Haven't you ever read this Scripture: «The stone that the builders rejected has become the cornerstone.  <sup>&#123;Or capstone&#125;</sup>

**11** This was the Lord's doing, and it is amazing in our eyes'?"  <sup>&#123;Ps 118:22-23&#125;</sup>

**12** They were trying to arrest him but were afraid of the crowd. Realizing that he had spoken this parable against them, they left him alone and went away.

**13** <sup>&#123;A Question about Paying Taxes&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 22:15-22; Luke 20:20-26)&#125;</sup> d Then they sent some Pharisees and some Herodians <sup>&#123;I.e. Royal party sympathizers&#125;</sup> to him, intending to trap him in what he said.

**14** They came and told him, "Teacher, we know that you are sincere. You don't favor any individual, because you pay no attention to external appearance. Rather, you teach the way of God truthfully. Is it lawful to pay taxes to Caesar or not? Should we pay them or should we not?"

**15** Seeing through their hypocrisy, Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> replied to them, "Why are you testing me? Bring me a denarius and let me look at it."

**16** So they brought one. Then he asked them, "Whose face and name are on this?" They told him, "Caesar's."

**17** So Jesus told them, "Give back to Caesar the things that are Caesar's, and to God the things that are God's." And they were utterly amazed at him.

**18** <sup>&#123;A Question about the Resurrection&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 22:23-33; Luke 20:27-40)&#125;</sup> d Then some Sadducees, who claim there is no resurrection, came to Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. him&#125;</sup> and asked him,

**19** "Teacher, Moses wrote for us that if a man's brother dies and leaves a wife but no child, he should marry the widow and have children for his brother. <sup>&#123;Deut 25:5-6&#125;</sup>

**20** There were seven brothers. The first one married and died without having children.

**21** Then the second married her and died without having children, and so did the third.

**22** None of the seven left any children. Last of all, the woman died, too.

**23** In the resurrection, whose wife will she be, since all seven had married her?" <sup>&#123;Lit. had her as wife&#125;</sup>

**24** Jesus answered them, "Aren't you mistaken because you don't know the Scriptures or God's power?

**25** When people <sup>&#123;Lit. they&#125;</sup> rise from the dead, they neither marry nor are given in marriage but are like the angels in heaven.
