**26** As for the dead being raised, haven't you read in the book of Moses, in the story about the bush, how God said, «I am the God of Abraham, the God of Isaac, and the God of Jacob'?  <sup>&#123;Exod 3:6&#125;</sup>

**27** He is not the God of the dead, but of the living. You are badly mistaken!"

**28** <sup>&#123;The Greatest Commandment&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 22:34-40; Luke 10:25-28)&#125;</sup> d Then one of the scribes came near and heard the Sadducees <sup>&#123;Lit. heard them&#125;</sup> arguing with one another. He saw how well Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> answered them, so he asked him, "Which commandment is the most important of them all?"

**29** Jesus answered, "The most important is, «Hear, O Israel, the Lord our God is one Lord,

**30** and you must love the Lord your God with all your heart, with all your soul, with all your mind, and with all your strength.' <sup>&#123;Deut 6:4-5&#125;</sup>

**31** The second is this: «You must love your neighbor as yourself.'<sup>&#123;Lev 19:18&#125;</sup> No other commandment is greater than these."

**32** Then the scribe told him, "Well said, <sup>&#123;The Gk. lacks said&#125;</sup> Teacher! You have told the truth that «God <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> is one, and there is no other besides him.'<sup>&#123;Deut 6:4&#125;</sup>

**33** To love him with all your heart, with all your understanding, and with all your strength, and to love your neighbor as yourself is more important than all the burnt offerings and sacrifices."

**34** When Jesus saw how wisely the man <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> answered, he told him, "You are not far from the kingdom of God." After that, no one dared to ask him another question.

**35** <sup>&#123;A Question about David's Son&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 22:41-46; Luke 20:41-44)&#125;</sup> d While Jesus was teaching in the Temple, he asked, "How can the scribes say that the Messiah <sup>&#123;Or Christ&#125;</sup> is David's son?

**36** David himself said by the Holy Spirit, «The Lord told my Lord, "Sit at my right hand, until I put your enemies under your feet."' <sup>&#123;Ps 110:1; other mss read until I make your enemies a footstool for your feet."'&#125;</sup>

**37** David himself calls him «Lord,'so how can he be his son?" And the large crowd kept listening to him with delight.

**38** <sup>&#123;Jesus Denounces the Scribes&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 23:1-36; Luke 20:45-47)&#125;</sup> d As he taught, he said, "Beware of the scribes! They like to walk around in long robes, to be greeted in the marketplaces,

**39** and to have the best seats in the synagogues and the places of honor at banquets.

**40** They devour widows'houses <sup>&#123;I.e. rob widows by taking their houses&#125;</sup> and say long prayers to cover it up. They will receive greater condemnation!"

**41** <sup>&#123;The Widow's Offering&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Luke 21:1-4)&#125;</sup> d As Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> sat facing the offering box, he watched how the crowd was dropping their money into it. <sup>&#123;Lit. into the offering box&#125;</sup> Many rich people were dropping in large amounts.

**42** Then a destitute widow came and dropped in two small copper coins, <sup>&#123;Lit. lepta , the smallest coin denominated in the 1 st century Jewish economy&#125;</sup> worth about a cent. <sup>&#123;Lit. quadrans , worth of the Roman assarion coin, or about 1/10 of a day's wage&#125;</sup>

**43** He called his disciples and told them, "I tell all of you <sup>&#123;The Gk. pronoun you is pl.&#125;</sup> with certainty, this destitute widow has dropped in more than everyone who is contributing to the offering box,

**44** because all of them contributed out of their surplus, but out of her poverty she has given everything she had to live on."

## Chapter 13

**1** <sup>&#123;Jesus Predicts the Destruction of the Temple&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 24:1-2; Luke 21:5-6)&#125;</sup> d As Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> was leaving the Temple, one of his disciples told him, "Look, Teacher, what large stones and what beautiful buildings!"

**2** "Do you see these large buildings?" Jesus responded. "Not one stone here will be left on another that will not be torn down."

**3** <sup>&#123;Cults, Revolutions, Famines, and Earthquakes&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 24:3-14; Luke 21:7-19)&#125;</sup> d As Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> was sitting on the Mount of Olives facing the Temple, Peter, James, John, and Andrew were asking him privately,

**4** "Tell us, when will these things happen, and what will be the sign when these things will be put into effect?"

**5** Jesus began to say to them, "See to it that no one deceives you.

**6** Many will come in my name and say, «I AM,'and they will deceive many people.  <sup>&#123;The Gk. lacks people&#125;</sup>

**7** But when you hear of wars and rumors of wars, stop being alarmed. These things must take place, but the end hasn't come yet,

**8** because nation will rise up in arms against nation, and kingdom against kingdom. There will be earthquakes and famines in various places. These things are only the beginning of the birth pains."

**9** <sup>&#123;Future Persecution and Evangelism&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 10:16-25; Matthew 24:9-14; Luke 21:12-19)&#125;</sup> d "As for yourselves, be on your guard! People <sup>&#123;Lit. They&#125;</sup> will hand you over to local councils, and you will be beaten in their synagogues. You will stand before governors and kings to testify to them because of me.

**10** But first, the gospel must be proclaimed to all nations.  <sup>&#123;Or gentiles&#125;</sup>

**11** "When they take you away and hand you over for trial, don't worry ahead of time about what you will say. Instead, say whatever is given to you at that time, because it won't be you speaking, but the Holy Spirit.

**12** Brother will betray brother to death, and a father his child. Children will rebel against their parents and have them put to death.

**13** You will be hated continuously by everyone because of my name. But the person who endures to the end will be saved."

**14** <sup>&#123;Signs of the End&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 24:15-28; Luke 21:20-24)&#125;</sup> d "So when you see the destructive desecration standing where it should not be (let the reader take note) , <sup>&#123;Dan 9:27; 11:31; 12:11&#125;</sup> then those who are in Judea must flee to the mountains.

**15** The one who is on his housetop must not come down and go into his house to take anything out of it,

**16** and the one who is in the field must not turn back to get his coat.

**17** "How terrible it will be for women who are pregnant or who are nursing babies in those days!

**18** Pray that it may not be in winter,

**19** because those days will be a time of suffering, <sup>&#123;Or tribulation&#125;</sup> a kind that has not happened from the beginning of God's creation until now, and certainly will never happen again.

**20** If the Lord had not shortened those days, no one would be saved. But for the sake of the elect whom he has chosen, he has shortened those days.

**21** "At that time, if anyone says to you, «Look! Here is the Messiah!', <sup>&#123;Or Christ&#125;</sup> or, «Look! There he is!', don't believe it,

**22** because false messiahs <sup>&#123;Or christs&#125;</sup> and false prophets will appear and produce signs and omens to deceive, if possible, the elect.

**23** So be on your guard! I've told you everything before it happens."

**24** <sup>&#123;The Coming of the Son of Man&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 24:29-31; Luke 21:25-28)&#125;</sup> d "But after the suffering of those days, «The sun will be darkened, the moon will not give its light,

**25** the stars will fall from the sky, and the powers of heaven will be shaken loose.' <sup>&#123;Isa 13:10; 34:4; Joel 2:10&#125;</sup>

**26** Then people <sup>&#123;Lit. they&#125;</sup> will see «the Son of Man coming in clouds'<sup>&#123;Dan 7:13&#125;</sup> with great power and glory.

**27** He will send out his angels and gather his elect from the four winds, from the ends of the earth to the ends of heaven."

**28** <sup>&#123;The Lesson from the Fig Tree&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 24:32-35; Luke 21:29-33)&#125;</sup> d "Now learn a lesson <sup>&#123;Or parable&#125;</sup> from the fig tree. When its branches become tender and it produces leaves, you know that summer is near.

**29** In the same way, when you see these things taking place, you will know that the Son of Man <sup>&#123;Lit. that he&#125;</sup> is near, right at the door.

**30** I tell all of you <sup>&#123;The Gk. pronoun you is pl.&#125;</sup> with certainty, this generation will not disappear until all these things take place.

**31** Heaven and earth will disappear, but my words will never disappear."

**32** <sup>&#123;The Unknown Day and Hour of the Messiah's Return&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 24:36-44)&#125;</sup> d "No one knows when that day or hour will come <sup>&#123;Lit. about that day and hour&#125;</sup> not the angels in heaven, not the Son, but only the Father.

**33** Be careful! Watch out! Because you don't know when the time will come.

**34** It's like a man who went on a trip. As he left home, he put his servants in charge, each with his own work, and he ordered the doorkeeper to be alert.

**35** So keep on watching, because you don't know when the master of the house is coming whether in the evening, at three o'clock in the morning, <sup>&#123;Lit. at cock crow&#125;</sup> or at dawn.

**36** Otherwise, he may come suddenly and find you asleep.

**37** I'm telling you what I'm telling everyone: Be alert!"

## Chapter 14

**1** <sup>&#123;The Plot to Kill Jesus&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 26:1-5; Luke 22:1-2; John 11:45-53)&#125;</sup> d Now it was two days before the Passover and the Festival of Unleavened Bread. The high priests and the scribes were looking for a way to arrest Jesus secretly and to have him put to death,

**2** because they kept saying, "This must not happen during the festival. Otherwise, there'll be a riot among the people."

**3** <sup>&#123;A Woman Anoints Jesus&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 26:6-13; John 12:1-8)&#125;</sup> d While Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> was in Bethany sitting at the table in the home of Simon the leper, a woman arrived with an alabaster jar of very expensive perfume made from pure nard. She broke open the jar and poured the perfume on his head.

**4** Irritated, some who were there asked one another, "Why was the perfume wasted like this?

**5** This perfume could have been sold for more than 300 denarii <sup>&#123;A denarius was the average day's wage for a laborer.&#125;</sup> and the money <sup>&#123;The Gk. lacks the money&#125;</sup> given to the destitute." So they got extremely angry with her.

**6** But Jesus said, "Leave her alone. Why are you bothering her? She has done a beautiful thing for me,

**7** because you will always have the destitute with you and can help them whenever you want, but you will not always have me.

**8** She has done what she could. She poured perfume on my body in preparation for my burial.

**9** I tell all of you <sup>&#123;The Gk. pronoun you is pl.&#125;</sup> with certainty, wherever the gospel is proclaimed in the whole world, what she has done will also be told as a memorial to her."

**10** <sup>&#123;Judas Agrees to Betray Jesus&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 26:14-16; Luke 22:3-6)&#125;</sup> d Then Judas Iscariot, one of the Twelve, went to the high priests to betray Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. him&#125;</sup> to them.

**11** After they had listened to him, <sup>&#123;The Gk. lacks to him&#125;</sup> they were delighted and promised to give him money. So he began to look for a good opportunity to betray him.

**12** <sup>&#123;The Passover with the Disciples&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 26:17-25; Luke 22:7-14, 21-23; John 13:21-30)&#125;</sup> d On the first day of the Festival <sup>&#123;The Gk. lacks of the Festival&#125;</sup> of Unleavened Bread, when the Passover lamb is sacrificed, Jesus'<sup>&#123;Lit. his&#125;</sup> disciples asked him, "Where do you want us to go and make preparations for you to eat the Passover meal?"

**13** He sent two of his disciples, telling them, "Go into the city, and you will meet a man carrying a jug of water. Follow him.

**14** When he goes into a house, <sup>&#123;Lit. Wherever he enters&#125;</sup> say to its owner that the Teacher asks, «Where is my room where I can eat the Passover meal with my disciples?»

**15** Then he will show you a large upstairs room that is furnished and ready. Get everything ready for us there."

**16** So the disciples left and went into the city. They found everything just as Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> had told them, and they prepared the Passover meal.

**17** When evening came, Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> arrived with the Twelve.

**18** While they were at the table eating, Jesus said, "I tell all of you <sup>&#123;The Gk. pronoun you is pl.&#125;</sup> with certainty, one of you is going to betray me, one who is eating with me."

**19** They began to be very sad and asked him, one after the other, "Surely I am not the one, am I?"

**20** He told them, "It's one of you Twelve, the one who is dipping his bread into the bowl with me.

**21** For the Son of Man is going away, just as it has been written about him, but how terrible it will be for that man by whom the Son of Man is betrayed! It would have been better for him if he had never been born."

**22** <sup>&#123;The Lord's Supper&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 26:26-30; Luke 22:15-20)&#125;</sup> d While they were eating, Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> took a loaf of bread and blessed it. Then he broke it in pieces and handed it to them, saying, "Take some. This is my body."

**23** Then he took a cup, gave thanks, and gave it to them, and they all drank from it.

**24** He told them, "This is my blood of the covenant that is being poured out for many people.

**25** I tell all of you <sup>&#123;The Gk. pronoun you is pl.&#125;</sup> with certainty, I will never again drink the product of the vine until that day when I drink it new in the kingdom of God."

**26** After singing a hymn, they went out to the Mount of Olives.

**27** <sup>&#123;Jesus Predicts Peter's Denial&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 26:31-35; Luke 22:31-34; John 13:36-38)&#125;</sup> d Then Jesus told them, "All of you will turn against me, because it is written, «I will strike the shepherd, and the sheep will be scattered.' <sup>&#123;Zech 13:7&#125;</sup>

**28** However, after I have been raised, I will go to Galilee ahead of you."

**29** But Peter told him, "Even if everyone else turns against you, I certainly won't."

**30** Jesus told him, "I tell you <sup>&#123;The Gk. pronoun you is sing.&#125;</sup> with certainty, today, this very night, before a rooster crows twice, you will deny me three times."

**31** But Peter <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> kept saying emphatically, "Even if I have to die with you, I will never deny you!" And all the others kept saying the same thing.

**32** <sup>&#123;Jesus Prays in the Garden of Gethsemane&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 26:36-46; Luke 22:39-46)&#125;</sup> d Then they came to a place called Gethsemane, and he told his disciples, "Sit down here while I pray."

**33** He took Peter, James, and John along with him, and he began to feel distressed and troubled.

**34** So he told them, "I am deeply grieved, even to the point of death. Wait here and stay awake."

**35** Going on a little farther, he fell to the ground and kept praying that if it were possible the hour might pass from him.

**36** He kept repeating, "Abba! <sup>&#123;Abba is Heb./Aram. for Father&#125;</sup> Father! All things are possible for you. Take this cup away from me. Yet not what I want but what you want."

**37** When he went back, he found his disciples <sup>&#123;Lit. found them&#125;</sup> asleep. "Simon, are you asleep?" he asked Peter. "You couldn't stay awake for one hour, could you?

**38** All of you must stay awake and pray that you won't be tempted. The spirit is indeed willing, but the body <sup>&#123;Lit. flesh&#125;</sup> is weak."

**39** He went away again and prayed the same prayer as before. <sup>&#123;Lit. the same word&#125;</sup>

**40** Again he came back and found them asleep, because they could not keep their eyes open. They didn't even know what they should say to him.

**41** He came back a third time. "Are you still sleeping and resting?" <sup>&#123;Or You might as well keep on sleeping and resting.&#125;</sup> he asked. "Enough of that! The time has come. Look! The Son of Man is being betrayed into the hands of sinners.

**42** Get up! Let's go! See, the one who is betraying me is near!"

**43** <sup>&#123;Jesus is Arrested&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 26:47-56; Luke 22:47-53; John 18:3-12)&#125;</sup> d Just then, while Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> was still speaking, Judas, one of the Twelve, arrived. A crowd armed with swords and clubs was with him. They were from the high priests, the scribes, and the elders.

**44** Now the betrayer personally had given them a signal, saying, "The one I kiss <sup>&#123;People customarily greeted their friends with a kiss.&#125;</sup> is the man. Arrest him, and lead him safely away."

**45** So Judas <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> immediately went up to Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. him&#125;</sup> and said, "Rabbi,"<sup>&#123;Other mss. read Rabbi, Rabbi. The word is Heb. for Master and/or Teacher&#125;</sup> and kissed him tenderly.

**46** Then the men <sup>&#123;Lit. they&#125;</sup> took hold of Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. him&#125;</sup> and arrested him.

**47** But one of those standing there drew his sword and struck the high priest's servant, cutting off his ear.

**48** Jesus asked them, "Have you come out with swords and clubs to arrest me as if I were a bandit? <sup>&#123;Or revolutionary&#125;</sup>

**49** Day after day I was with you in the Temple teaching, yet you didn't arrest me. But the Scriptures must be fulfilled."

**50** Then all the disciples <sup>&#123;Lit. all of them&#125;</sup> deserted Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. him&#125;</sup> and ran away.

**51** <sup>&#123;The Young Man who Ran Away&#125;</sup> d A certain young man, who was wearing nothing but a linen sheet, was following Jesus. <sup>&#123;Lit. him&#125;</sup> When the men <sup>&#123;Lit. they&#125;</sup> grabbed him,

**52** he left the linen sheet behind and ran away naked.

**53** <sup>&#123;Jesus is Tried before the High Priest&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 26:57-68; Luke 22:54-55; John 18:13-14, 19-24)&#125;</sup> d Then they took Jesus to the high priest. All the high priests, elders, and scribes had gathered together.

**54** Peter followed Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. him&#125;</sup> at a distance as far as the high priest's courtyard. He was sitting with the servants and warming himself at the fire.

**55** Meanwhile, the high priests and the whole Council <sup>&#123;Or Sanhedrin&#125;</sup> were looking for some testimony against Jesus in order to have him put to death, but they couldn't find any.

**56** Although many people gave false testimony against him, their testimony didn't agree.

**57** Then some men stood up and gave false testimony against him, saying,

**58** "We ourselves heard him say, «I will destroy this sanctuary made by human <sup>&#123;The Gk. lacks human&#125;</sup> hands, and in three days I will build another one not made by human <sup>&#123;The Gk. lacks human&#125;</sup> hands.»"

**59** But even on this point their testimony didn't agree.

**60** Then the high priest stood up before them <sup>&#123;Lit. in the middle&#125;</sup> and asked Jesus, "Don't you have any answer to what these men are testifying against you?"

**61** But he kept silent and didn't answer at all. The high priest asked him again, "Are you the Messiah, <sup>&#123;Or Christ&#125;</sup> the Son of the Blessed One?"

**62** Jesus said, "I AM, and «you will see the Son of Man seated at the right hand of the Power' <sup>&#123;Ps 110:1&#125;</sup> and «coming with the clouds of heaven.»" <sup>&#123;Dan 7:13&#125;</sup>

**63** Then the high priest tore his clothes. "Why do we still need witnesses?" he asked.

**64** "You have heard his blasphemy! What is your verdict?" All of them condemned him as deserving death.

**65** Some of them began to spit on him. They blindfolded him and kept hitting him with their fists and telling him, "Prophesy!" Even the servants took him and slapped him around.

**66** <sup>&#123;Peter Denies Jesus&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 26:69-75; Luke 22:56-62; John 18:15-18, 25-27)&#125;</sup> d While Peter was down in the courtyard, one of the high priest's servant girls came by.

**67** When she saw Peter warming himself, she glared at him and said, "You, too, were with Jesus from Nazareth."

**68** But he denied it, saying, "I don't know or even understand what you're talking about!" Then he went out into the entryway. Just then a rooster crowed. <sup>&#123;Other mss. lack Just then a rooster crowed&#125;</sup>

**69** The servant girl saw him and again told those who were standing around, "This man is one of them!"

**70** Again he denied it. After a little while, the people who were standing there began to say to Peter again, "Obviously you're one of them, because you are a Galilean!"

**71** Then he began to invoke a divine curse and to swear with an oath, "I don't know this man you're talking about!"

**72** Just then a rooster crowed a second time. Peter remembered that Jesus told him, "Before a rooster crows twice, you will deny me three times." Then he broke down and cried.

## Chapter 15

**1** <sup>&#123;Jesus is Taken to Pilate&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 27:1-2, 11-14; Luke 23:1-5; John 18:28-38)&#125;</sup> d As soon as it was morning, the high priests convened a meeting with the elders and scribes and the whole Council. <sup>&#123;Or Sanhedrin&#125;</sup> They bound Jesus with chains, led him away, and handed him over to Pilate.

**2** Pilate asked him, "Are you the king of the Jews?" Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. He&#125;</sup> answered him, "You say so."

**3** The high priests kept accusing him of many things.

**4** So Pilate asked him again, "Don't you have any answer? Look how many accusations they're bringing against you!"

**5** But since Jesus no longer answered, Pilate was astonished.

**6** <sup>&#123;Jesus is Sentenced to Death&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 27:15-26; Luke 23:13-25; John 18:39-19:16)&#125;</sup> d At every festival, <sup>&#123;I.e. Passover&#125;</sup> Pilate <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> would release any one prisoner whom the people <sup>&#123;Lit. they&#125;</sup> requested.

**7** Now there was a man in prison named Barabbas. He had been with the insurgents who had committed murder during the rebellion.

**8** So the crowd came and began to request that Pilate <sup>&#123;The Gk. lacks Pilate&#125;</sup> do for them what he always did. <sup>&#123;I.e. release one condemned prisoner during Passover&#125;</sup>

**9** Pilate answered them, "Do you want me to release the king of the Jews for you?"

**10** because he knew that the high priests had handed him over due to jealousy.

**11** But the high priests stirred up the crowd to get him to release Barabbas for them instead.

**12** So Pilate asked them again, "Then what should I do with the man you call <sup>&#123;Other mss. lack the man you call&#125;</sup> «the King of the Jews'?"

**13** They shouted back, "Crucify him!"

**14** Pilate asked them, "Why? What has he done wrong?" But they shouted even louder, "Crucify him!"

**15** So Pilate, wanting to satisfy the crowd, released Barabbas for them, but he had Jesus whipped and handed over to be crucified.

**16** <sup>&#123;The Soldiers Make Fun of Jesus&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 27:27-31; John 19:2-3)&#125;</sup> d The soldiers led Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. him&#125;</sup> into the courtyard of the palace (that is, the governor's headquarters) <sup>&#123;Lit. praetorium&#125;</sup> and called out the whole guard.

**17** They dressed him in a purple robe, twisted some thorns into a victor's crown, and placed it on his head. <sup>&#123;Lit. on him&#125;</sup>

**18** They began to greet him, "Long live the king of the Jews!"

**19** They kept hitting him on the head with a stick, spitting on him, kneeling in front of him, and worshiping him.

**20** When they had finished making fun of him, they stripped him of the purple robe, put his own clothes back on him, and led him away to crucify him.

**21** <sup>&#123;Jesus is Crucified&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 27:32-44; Luke 23:26-43; John 19:17-27)&#125;</sup> d They forced a certain passer-by named <sup>&#123;The Gk. lacks named&#125;</sup> Simon of Cyrene, the father of Alexander and Rufus, who happened to be coming in from the country, to carry Jesus'<sup>&#123;Lit. his&#125;</sup> cross.

**22** They took Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. him&#125;</sup> to a place called Golgotha, which means Skull Place.

**23** They tried to give him wine mixed with myrrh, but he wouldn't accept it.

**24** Then they crucified him. They divided his clothes among themselves by throwing dice to see what each one would get.

**25** It was nine in the morning <sup>&#123;Lit. the third hour&#125;</sup> when they crucified him.

**26** The written notice of the charge against him read, "The king of the Jews."

**27** They crucified two bandits <sup>&#123;Or revolutionaries&#125;</sup> with him, one on his right and the other on his left. <sup>&#123;Other mss. read on his left.&#125;</sup>

**28** Then the Scripture was fulfilled that says, "He was counted with criminals."

**29** Those who passed by kept insulting <sup>&#123;Or blaspheming&#125;</sup> him, shaking their heads, and saying, "Ha! You who were going to destroy the sanctuary and rebuild it in three days

**30** save yourself and come down from the cross!"

**31** In the same way, the high priests, along with the scribes, were also making fun of him among themselves. They kept saying, "He saved others but can't save himself!

**32** Let the Messiah, <sup>&#123;Or Christ&#125;</sup> the king of Israel, come down from the cross now, since seeing is believing!" Even the men who were crucified with him kept insulting him.

**33** <sup>&#123;Jesus Dies on the Cross&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 27:45-56; Luke 23:44-49; John 19:28-30)&#125;</sup> d At noon, <sup>&#123;Lit. At the sixth hour&#125;</sup> darkness came over the whole land <sup>&#123;Or earth&#125;</sup> until three in the afternoon. <sup>&#123;Lit. the ninth hour&#125;</sup>

**34** At three o'clock, <sup>&#123;Lit. the ninth hour&#125;</sup> Jesus cried out with a loud voice, "Eloi, eloi, <sup>&#123;Eloi, eloi are Gk. transliterations for the Heb. My God, my God in Ps 22:1&#125;</sup> lema sabachthani?" , <sup>&#123;lema sabachthani is a Gk. transliteration for the Aram. rendering of the Heb. in Ps 22:1, which means Why have you forsaken me?&#125;</sup> which means, "My God, my God, why have you forsaken me?" <sup>&#123;Ps 22:1&#125;</sup>

**35** When some of the people standing there heard this, they said, "Listen! He's calling for Elijah!" <sup>&#123;Elijah in Heb. sounds like Eloi .&#125;</sup>

**36** So someone ran and soaked a sponge in some sour wine. Then he put it on a stick and offered Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. him&#125;</sup> a drink, saying, "Wait! Let's see if Elijah comes to take him down!"

**37** Then Jesus gave a loud cry and breathed his last.

**38** The curtain <sup>&#123;This curtain separated the Holy Place from the Most Holy Place.&#125;</sup> in the sanctuary was torn in two from top to bottom.

**39** When the centurion <sup>&#123;A Roman centurion commanded about 100 men.&#125;</sup> who stood facing Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. him&#125;</sup> saw how he had cried out and <sup>&#123;Other mss. lack cried out and&#125;</sup> breathed his last, he said, "This man certainly was the Son of God!"

**40** Now there were women watching from a distance. Among them were Mary Magdalene, <sup>&#123;Or Mary of Magdala&#125;</sup> Mary the mother of young James and Joseph, and Salome.

**41** They used to accompany him and care for him while he was in Galilee. Many other women who had come up to Jerusalem with him were there, too.

**42** <sup>&#123;Jesus is Buried&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 27:57-61; Luke 23:50-56; John 19:38-42)&#125;</sup> d It was the Day of Preparation, that is, the day before the Sabbath. Since it was already evening,

**43** Joseph of Arimathea, a highly respected member of the Council, <sup>&#123;Or Sanhedrin&#125;</sup> who was waiting for the kingdom of God, went boldly to Pilate and asked for the body of Jesus.

**44** Pilate was amazed to hear <sup>&#123;The Gk. lacks to hear&#125;</sup> that Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> had already died, so he summoned the centurion to ask him if he was in fact dead.

**45** When he learned from the centurion that he was dead, he let Joseph have the corpse.

**46** Joseph <sup>&#123;Lit. He&#125;</sup> bought some linen cloth, took the body <sup>&#123;Lit. it&#125;</sup> down, wrapped it in the cloth, laid it in a tomb that had been cut out of the rock, and rolled a stone against the door of the tomb.

**47** Now Mary Magdalene <sup>&#123;Or Mary of Magdala&#125;</sup> and Mary the mother of Joseph observed where the body <sup>&#123;Lit. where it&#125;</sup> had been laid.

## Chapter 16

**1** <sup>&#123;Jesus is Raised from the Dead&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 28:1-8; Luke 24:1-12; John 20:1-10)&#125;</sup> d When the Sabbath was over, Mary Magdalene, <sup>&#123;Or Mary of Magdala&#125;</sup> Mary the mother of James, and Salome bought spices to go and anoint Jesus. <sup>&#123;Lit. him&#125;</sup>

**2** Very early on the first day of the week, <sup>&#123;Lit. first of the Sabbaths&#125;</sup> when the sun had just come up, they were going to the tomb.

**3** They kept saying to one another, "Who will roll away the stone for us from the entrance to the tomb?"

**4** Then they looked up and saw that the stone had been rolled away. (It was a very large stone.)

**5** As they went into the tomb, they saw a young man dressed in a white robe sitting on the right side, and they were utterly astonished.

**6** But he told them, "Stop being astonished! You are looking for Jesus of Nazareth, who was crucified. He has been raised. He is not here. Look at the place where they laid him.

**7** But go and tell his disciples, especially Peter, that Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> is going ahead of you to Galilee. There you will see him, just as he told you."

**8** So they left the tomb and ran away, overwhelmed by shock and astonishment. They didn't say a thing to anyone, because they were afraid. <sup>&#123;Some mss. end Mark here; others include verses 9-20. Some mss. conclude the book with the following shorter ending (others include the shorter ending and then continue with verses 9-20): They reported to those who were with Peter everything they had been commanded. After this, Jesus sent out through them, from east to west, the sacred and indestructible message of eternal salvation. Amen.&#125;</sup>

**9** <sup>&#123;Jesus Appears to Mary Magdalene&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 28:9-10; John 20:11-18)&#125;</sup> d After Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> had risen early on the first day of that week, <sup>&#123;Lit. first Sabbath&#125;</sup> he appeared first to Mary Magdalene, <sup>&#123;Or Mary of Magdala&#125;</sup> from whom he had driven out seven demons.

**10** She went and told those who had been with Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. him&#125;</sup> and who now were grieving and crying.

**11** When they heard that he was alive and that he had been seen by her, they refused to believe Mary. <sup>&#123;Lit. her&#125;</sup>

**12** <sup>&#123;Jesus Appears to Two Disciples&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Luke 24:13-35)&#125;</sup> d After this, Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> appeared in a different form to two disciples <sup>&#123;Lit. two of them&#125;</sup> as they were walking into the country.

**13** They went back and told the others, who didn't believe them, either.

**14** <sup>&#123;Jesus Commissions His Disciples&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 28:16-20; Luke 24:36-49; John 20:19-23; Acts 1:6-8)&#125;</sup> d Finally he appeared to his eleven disciples <sup>&#123;The Gk. lacks disciples&#125;</sup> while they were eating. He rebuked them for their unbelief and stubbornness, because they had not believed those who had seen him after he had risen.

**15** Then he told them, "As you go into all the world, proclaim the gospel to everyone. <sup>&#123;Lit. to the whole creation&#125;</sup>

**16** Whoever believes and is baptized will be saved, but whoever doesn't believe will be condemned.

**17** "These are the signs that will accompany those who believe: In my name they will drive out demons; they will speak in new languages;  <sup>&#123;Other mss. lack with their hands&#125;</sup>

**18** they will pick up snakes with their hands; <sup>&#123;Or new tongues&#125;</sup> even if they drink any deadly poison it will not hurt them; and they will place their hands on the sick, and they will recover."

**19** <sup>&#123;Jesus is Taken Up to Heaven&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Luke 24:50-53; Acts 1:9-11)&#125;</sup> d So the Lord Jesus, <sup>&#123;Other mss. lack Jesus&#125;</sup> after talking with his disciples, <sup>&#123;Lit. with them&#125;</sup> was taken up to heaven and sat down at the right hand of God.

**20** Then his disciples <sup>&#123;Lit. Then they&#125;</sup> went out and preached everywhere, while the Lord kept working with them and confirming the message by the signs that accompanied it.

---

# Matthew (ISV)

## Chapter 1

**1** <sup>&#123;The New Testament&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;The Gospel According to&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;Matthew&#125;</sup> d <sup>&#123;An Introduction to Jesus the Messiah&#125;</sup> d This is <sup>&#123;The Gk. lacks This is&#125;</sup> a record of the life <sup>&#123;Or birth&#125;</sup> of Jesus the Messiah, the son of David, the son of Abraham.

**2** <sup>&#123;The Ancestry of Jesus&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Luke 3:23-28)&#125;</sup> d Abraham fathered Isaac, Isaac fathered Jacob, and Jacob fathered Judah and his brothers.

**3** Judah fathered Perez and Zerah by Tamar, Perez fathered Hezron, Hezron fathered Aram,

**4** Aram fathered Amminadab, Amminadab fathered Nahshon, and Nahshon fathered Salmon.

**5** Salmon fathered Boaz by Rahab, Boaz fathered Obed by Ruth, Obed fathered Jesse,

**6** and Jesse fathered King David. David fathered Solomon by the wife of Uriah,

**7** Solomon fathered Rehoboam, Rehoboam fathered Abijah, Abijah fathered Asaph, <sup>&#123;Other mss. read Asa ; cf. 1Chr 3:10&#125;</sup>

**8** Asaph <sup>&#123;Other mss. read Asa ; cf. 1Chr 3:10&#125;</sup> fathered Jehoshaphat, Jehoshaphat fathered Joram, Joram fathered Uzziah,

**9** Uzziah fathered Jotham, Jotham fathered Ahaz, Ahaz fathered Hezekiah,

**10** Hezekiah fathered Manasseh, Manasseh fathered Amos, <sup>&#123;Other mss. read Amon ; cf. 1Chr 3:13&#125;</sup> and Amos <sup>&#123;Other mss. read Amon ; cf. 1Chr 3:13&#125;</sup> fathered Josiah.

**11** Josiah fathered Jechoniah <sup>&#123;Or Jeconiah ; cf. Jer 22:28-30&#125;</sup> and his brothers at the time of the deportation to Babylon.

**12** After the deportation to Babylon, Jechoniah <sup>&#123;Or Jeconiah ; cf. Jer 22:28-30&#125;</sup> fathered Salathiel, <sup>&#123;Or Shealtiel ; cf. 1Chr 3:17; Ezra 3:2,8; 5:2; Neh 12:1; Luke 3:27&#125;</sup> Salathiel fathered Zerubbabel,

**13** Zerubbabel fathered Abiud, Abiud fathered Eliakim, Eliakim fathered Azor,

**14** Azor fathered Zadok, Zadok fathered Achim, Achim fathered Eliud,

**15** Eliud fathered Eleazar, Eleazar fathered Matthan, and Matthan fathered Jacob.

**16** Jacob fathered Joseph, the husband of Mary, who was the mother of Jesus, <sup>&#123;Lit. of whom Jesus was born&#125;</sup> who is called the Messiah. <sup>&#123;Or Christ&#125;</sup>

**17** So all the generations from Abraham to David totaled fourteen <sup>&#123;I.e. the numerical value of the Heb. name David&#125;</sup> generations, and from David to the deportation to Babylon totaled fourteen generations, and from the deportation to Babylon to the Messiah <sup>&#123;Or Christ&#125;</sup> there were fourteen generations.

**18** <sup>&#123;The Birth of Jesus&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Luke 2:1-7)&#125;</sup> d Now the birth of Jesus the Messiah <sup>&#123;Or Christ&#125;</sup> happened in this way. When his mother Mary was engaged <sup>&#123;Engagement involved a legally binding promise of marriage.&#125;</sup> to Joseph, before they lived together she was discovered to be pregnant by the Holy Spirit.

**19** Her husband Joseph, being a righteous man and unwilling to disgrace her, decided to divorce her secretly.

**20** After he had thought about it, an angel of the Lord appeared to him in a dream. "Joseph, son of David,"he said, "don't be afraid to take Mary as your wife, because what has been conceived in her is from the Holy Spirit.

**21** She will give birth to a son, and you are to name him Jesus, <sup>&#123;The name Jesus means The Lord saves&#125;</sup> because he is the one who will save his people from their sins."

**22** Now all this happened to fulfill what was declared by the Lord through the prophet when he said,

**23** "See, a virgin will become pregnant and give birth to a son, and they will name him Immanuel,"<sup>&#123;Isa 7:14&#125;</sup> which means, "God with us."

**24** When Joseph got up from his sleep, he did as the angel of the Lord had commanded him and took Mary as <sup>&#123;The Gk. lacks Mary as&#125;</sup> his wife.

**25** He did not have marital relations with <sup>&#123;Lit. not know&#125;</sup> her until she had given birth to a son; <sup>&#123;Other mss. read to her firstborn son&#125;</sup> and he named him Jesus.

## Chapter 2

**1** <sup>&#123;The Visit of the Wise Men&#125;</sup> d After Jesus had been born in Bethlehem of Judea in the days of King Herod, wise men <sup>&#123;Lit. magoi (Magi); i.e. Aramaic speaking wise men from Mesopotamia; or magi-astrologers; and so throughout the chapter; cf. Dan 1:4&#125;</sup> from the east arrived in Jerusalem

**2** and asked, "Where is the one who was born king of the Jews? We saw his star in the east <sup>&#123;Or at its rising&#125;</sup> and have come to worship him."

**3** When King Herod heard this, he was disturbed, as was all of Jerusalem.

**4** He called together all the high priests and scribes of the people and asked them where the Messiah <sup>&#123;Or Christ&#125;</sup> was to be born.

**5** They told him, "In Bethlehem of Judea, because that is what was written by the prophet:

**6** «O Bethlehem in the land of Judah, you are by no means least among the rulers of Judah, because from you will come a ruler who will shepherd <sup>&#123;Or govern&#125;</sup> my people Israel.»" <sup>&#123;Mic 5:2; 2 Sam 5:2&#125;</sup>

**7** Then Herod secretly called together the wise men, found out from them the time the star had appeared,

**8** and sent them to Bethlehem. He told them, <sup>&#123;The Gk. lacks them&#125;</sup> "As you go, search carefully for the child. When you find him, tell me so that I, too, may go and worship him."

**9** After listening to the king, they set out, and the star they had seen in the east <sup>&#123;Or at its rising&#125;</sup> went ahead of them until it came and stopped over the place where the child was.

**10** When they saw the star, they were ecstatic with joy.

**11** After they went into the house and saw the child with his mother Mary, they fell down and worshipped him. Then they opened their treasure sacks and offered him gifts of gold, frankincense, and myrrh.

**12** Having been warned in a dream not to go back to Herod, they left for their own country by a different road.

**13** <sup>&#123;The Escape to Egypt&#125;</sup> d After they had gone, an angel of the Lord appeared to Joseph in a dream. "Get up, take the child and his mother, and flee to Egypt,"he said. "Stay there until I tell you, because Herod intends to search for the child and kill him."

**14** So Joseph <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> got up, took the child and his mother, and left at night for Egypt.

**15** He stayed there until Herod's death in order to fulfill what was declared by the Lord through the prophet when he said, "Out of Egypt I called my Son." <sup>&#123;Hos 11:1&#125;</sup>

**16** <sup>&#123;The Massacre of the Infants&#125;</sup> d Herod flew into a rage when he learned that he had been tricked by the wise men, so he ordered the execution of all the male children in Bethlehem and all its neighboring regions, who were two years old and younger, according to the time that he had determined from the wise men.

**17** Then what was declared by the prophet Jeremiah was fulfilled when he said,

**18** "A voice was heard in Ramah: wailing and great mourning. Rachel was crying for her children. She refused to be comforted, because they no longer existed." <sup>&#123;Jer 31:15&#125;</sup>

**19** <sup>&#123;The Return to Nazareth&#125;</sup> d But after Herod died, an angel of the Lord appeared in a dream to Joseph in Egypt.

**20** "Get up,"he said. "Take the child and his mother, and go to the land of Israel, because those who were trying to kill <sup>&#123;Lit. were seeking the life of&#125;</sup> the child are dead."

**21** So Joseph <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> got up, took the child and his mother, and went into the land of Israel.

**22** But when he heard that Archelaus was ruling over Judea in place of his father Herod, he was afraid to go there. After having been warned in a dream, he left for the region of Galilee

**23** and came and settled in a town called Nazareth in order to fulfill what was said by the prophets: "He will be called a Nazarene." <sup>&#123;The Gk. Nazoraios may be a word play between Heb. netser, meaning branch (see Isa 11:1), and the name Nazareth.&#125;</sup>

## Chapter 3

**1** <sup>&#123;John the Baptist Prepares the Way&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Mark 1:1-8; Luke 3:1-9, 15-17; John 1:19-28)&#125;</sup> d About this time, <sup>&#123;Lit. In those days&#125;</sup> John the Baptist appeared, preaching in the Judean wilderness

**2** and saying, "Repent, because the kingdom from <sup>&#123;Lit. of&#125;</sup> heaven is near!"

**3** He was the one the prophet Isaiah was referring to when he said, "He is a voice calling out in the wilderness: «Prepare the way for the Lord! Make his paths straight!»" <sup>&#123;Isa 40:3&#125;</sup>

**4** John had clothing made of camel's hair and wore <sup>&#123;The Gk. lacks wore&#125;</sup> a leather belt around his waist. His diet consisted of grasshoppers <sup>&#123;Or locust-shaped carob seed pods&#125;</sup> and wild honey.

**5** Then the people of <sup>&#123;The Gk. lacks the people of&#125;</sup> Jerusalem, all Judea, and the entire region along the Jordan began flocking to him,

**6** being baptized by him in the Jordan River while they confessed their sins.

**7** But when John <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> saw many Pharisees and Sadducees coming to where he was baptizing, <sup>&#123;Lit. to his baptism&#125;</sup> he told them, "You children of serpents! Who warned you to flee from the coming wrath?

**8** Produce fruit that is consistent with repentance!

**9** Don't think you can say to yourselves, «We have father Abraham!'because I tell you that God can raise up descendants for Abraham from these stones!

**10** The ax already lies against the roots of the trees. So every tree that isn't producing good fruit will be cut down and thrown into the fire.

**11** I am baptizing you with <sup>&#123;Or in&#125;</sup> water as a token of repentance, <sup>&#123;Lit. for repentance&#125;</sup> but the one who is coming after me is stronger than I am, and I am not worthy to carry his sandals. It is he who will baptize you with <sup>&#123;Or in&#125;</sup> the Holy Spirit and fire.

**12** His winnowing fork is in his hand. He will clean up his threshing floor and gather his grain into the barn, but he will burn the chaff with inextinguishable fire."

**13** <sup>&#123;Jesus is Baptized&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Mark 1:9-11; Luke 3:21-22)&#125;</sup> d Then Jesus came from Galilee to the Jordan to be baptized by John.

**14** But John tried to stop him, saying, "I need to be baptized by you, and are you coming to me?"

**15** But Jesus answered him, "Let it be this way for now, because this is the proper way for us to fulfill all righteousness." <sup>&#123;Dan 9:24&#125;</sup> At this, he permitted him to be baptized. <sup>&#123;The Gk. lacks to be baptized&#125;</sup>

**16** When Jesus had been baptized, he immediately came up out of the water. Suddenly, the heavens opened up for him, and he saw the Spirit of God descending like a dove and coming to rest on him.

**17** Then a voice from heaven said, "This is my Son, whom I love. I am pleased with him!"

## Chapter 4

**1** <sup>&#123;Jesus is Tempted by Satan&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Mark 1:12-13; Luke 4:1-13)&#125;</sup> d After this, Jesus was led by the Spirit into the wilderness to be tempted by the devil.

**2** After fasting for 40 days and 40 nights, he finally became hungry.

**3** Then the tempter came. "Since <sup>&#123;Or "If, as is the case,&#125;</sup> you are the Son of God,"he said, "tell these stones to become loaves of bread."

**4** But he answered, "It is written, «One must not live on bread alone, but on every word coming out of the mouth of God.»" <sup>&#123;Deut 8:3&#125;</sup>

**5** Then the devil took him to the Holy City <sup>&#123;I.e. Jerusalem&#125;</sup> and had him stand on the highest point of the Temple.

**6** He told Jesus, <sup>&#123;Lit. him&#125;</sup> "Since <sup>&#123;Or "If, as is the case,&#125;</sup> you are the Son of God, throw yourself down, because it is written, «God <sup>&#123;Lit. He&#125;</sup> will put his angels in charge of you,'and, «With their hands they will hold you up, so that you will never hit your foot against a rock.»" <sup>&#123;Ps 91:11-12&#125;</sup>

**7** Jesus responded to him, "It is also written, «You must not tempt the Lord your God.»" <sup>&#123;Deut 6:16&#125;</sup>

**8** Once more the devil took him to a very high mountain and showed him all the kingdoms of the world, along with their splendor.

**9** He told Jesus, <sup>&#123;Lit. him&#125;</sup> "I will give you all these things if you will bow down and worship me!"

**10** Then Jesus told him, "Go away, <sup>&#123;Other mss. read Get behind me&#125;</sup> Satan! Because it is written, «You must worship the Lord your God and serve only him.»" <sup>&#123;Deut 6:13&#125;</sup>

**11** So the devil left him, and angels came and began ministering to him.

**12** <sup>&#123;Jesus Begins His Ministry in Galilee&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Mark 1:14-15; Luke 4:14-15)&#125;</sup> d Now when Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> heard that John had been arrested, he went back to Galilee.

**13** He left Nazareth and went and settled in Capernaum by the sea, in the regions of Zebulun and Naphtali,

**14** in order to fulfill what was declared by the prophet Isaiah when he said,

**15** "O Land of Zebulun and Land of Naphtali, on the road to the sea, across the Jordan, Galilee of the unbelievers! <sup>&#123;Lit. gentiles ; i.e. unbelieving non-Jews&#125;</sup>

**16** The people living <sup>&#123;Lit. sitting&#125;</sup> in darkness have seen a great light, and for those living <sup>&#123;Lit. sitting&#125;</sup> in the land and shadow of death, a light has risen." <sup>&#123;Isa 9:1-2&#125;</sup>

**17** From then on, Jesus began to preach and to say, "Repent, because the kingdom from <sup>&#123;Lit. of&#125;</sup> heaven is near!"

**18** <sup>&#123;Jesus Calls Four Fishermen&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Mark 1:16-20; Luke 5:1-11)&#125;</sup> d While Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> was walking beside the Sea of Galilee, he saw two brothers Simon (also <sup>&#123;The Gk. lacks also&#125;</sup> called Peter) and his brother Andrew. They were casting a net into the sea, because they were fishermen.

**19** "Follow me," he told them, "and I will make you fishers of people!"

**20** So at once they left their nets and followed him.

**21** Going on from there he saw two other brothers James son of Zebedee and his brother John. They were in a boat with their father Zebedee repairing their nets. When he called them,

**22** they immediately left the boat and their father and followed him.

**23** <sup>&#123;Jesus Ministers to Many People&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Luke 6:17-19)&#125;</sup> d Then he went throughout Galilee, teaching in their synagogues, proclaiming the gospel of the kingdom, and healing every disease and every illness among the people.

**24** His fame spread throughout Syria, and people <sup>&#123;Lit. they&#125;</sup> brought to him everyone who was sick those afflicted with various diseases and pains, the demon-possessed, the epileptics, and the paralyzed and he healed them.

**25** Large crowds from Galilee, the Decapolis, <sup>&#123;Lit. the Ten Cities, a loose federation of ten cities strongly influenced by Greek culture&#125;</sup> Jerusalem, Judea, and from across the Jordan followed him.

## Chapter 5

**1** <sup>&#123;Jesus Teaches about the Kingdom&#125;</sup> d When Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> saw the crowds, he went up on the hill. After taking his seat, his disciples came to him,

**2** and he began <sup>&#123;Lit. he opened his mouth and began&#125;</sup> to teach them, saying:

**3** <sup>&#123;The Blessed Attitudes&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Luke 6:20-23)&#125;</sup> d "How blessed are those who are destitute in spirit, because the kingdom from <sup>&#123;Lit. of&#125;</sup> heaven belongs to them!

**4** "How blessed are those who mourn, because it is they who will be comforted!

**5** "How blessed are those who are humble, <sup>&#123;Or gentle&#125;</sup> because it is they who will inherit the earth!

**6** "How blessed are those who are hungry and thirsty for righteousness, <sup>&#123;Or justice&#125;</sup> because it is they who will be satisfied!

**7** "How blessed are those who are merciful, because it is they who will receive mercy!

**8** "How blessed are those who are pure in heart, because it is they who will see God!

**9** "How blessed are those who make peace, because it is they who will be called God's children!

**10** "How blessed are those who are persecuted for righteousness'sake, because the kingdom from <sup>&#123;Lit. of&#125;</sup> heaven belongs to them!

**11** "How blessed are you whenever people <sup>&#123;Lit. they&#125;</sup> insult you, persecute you, and say all sorts of evil things against you falsely <sup>&#123;Other mss. lack falsely&#125;</sup> because of me!

**12** Rejoice and be extremely glad, because your reward in heaven is great! That's how they persecuted the prophets who came before you."

**13** <sup>&#123;Salt and Light in the World&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Mark 9:50; Luke 14:34-35)&#125;</sup> d "You are the salt of the world. But if the salt should lose its taste, how can it be made salty again? It's good for nothing but to be thrown out and trampled on by people.

**14** "You are the light of the world. A city located on a hill can't be hidden.

**15** People <sup>&#123;Lit. They&#125;</sup> don't light a lamp and put it under a basket but on a lamp stand, and it gives light to everyone in the house.

**16** In the same way, let your light shine before people in such a way that they will see your good actions and glorify your Father in heaven."

**17** <sup>&#123;Jesus Fulfills the Law and the Prophets&#125;</sup> d "Do not think that I came to destroy the Law or the Prophets. I didn't come to destroy them, but to fulfill them,

**18** because I tell all of you <sup>&#123;The Gk. pronoun you is pl.&#125;</sup> with certainty that until heaven and earth disappear, not one letter <sup>&#123;Lit. one iota&#125;</sup> or one stroke of a letter will disappear from the Law until everything has been accomplished.

**19** So whoever sets aside <sup>&#123;Or breaks&#125;</sup> one of the least of these commandments and teaches others to do the same will be called least in the kingdom from <sup>&#123;Lit. of&#125;</sup> heaven. But whoever does them and teaches them will be called great in the kingdom from <sup>&#123;Lit. of&#125;</sup> heaven

**20** because I tell you, unless your righteousness greatly exceeds that of the scribes and Pharisees, you will never enter the kingdom from <sup>&#123;Lit. of&#125;</sup> heaven!"

**21** <sup>&#123;Teaching about Anger&#125;</sup> d "You have heard that it was told those who lived long ago, «You must not murder,' <sup>&#123;Exod 20:13; Deut 5:17&#125;</sup> and, «Whoever murders will be subject to punishment.'<sup>&#123;Exod 21:12; Lev 24:17&#125;</sup>

**22** But I say to you, anyone who is angry with his brother without a cause <sup>&#123;Other mss. lack without a cause&#125;</sup> will be subject to punishment. And whoever says to his brother «Raka!'<sup>&#123;Raka is Aram. for You worthless one&#125;</sup> will be subject to the Council. <sup>&#123;Or Sanhedrin&#125;</sup> And whoever says, «You fool!'will be subject to hell <sup>&#123;Lit. Gehenna ; a Gk. transliteration of the Heb. for Valley of Hinnom&#125;</sup> fire.

**23** "So if you are presenting your gift at the altar and remember there that your brother has something against you,

**24** leave your gift there before the altar and first go and be reconciled to your brother. Then come and offer your gift.

**25** Come to terms quickly with your opponent while you are on the way to court, <sup>&#123;Lit. while you are with him on the way&#125;</sup> or your opponent may hand you over to the judge, and the judge to the guard, and you will be thrown into prison.

**26** I tell you  <sup>&#123;The Gk. pronoun you is sing.&#125;</sup> with certainty, you will not get out of there until you pay back the last dollar!" <sup>&#123;Lit. quadran ; i.e. about 1/64 th of a daily wage for a common worker&#125;</sup>

**27** <sup>&#123;Teaching about Adultery&#125;</sup> d "You have heard that it was said, «You must not commit adultery.' <sup>&#123;Exod 20:14; Deut 5:18&#125;</sup>

**28** But I say to you, anyone who stares at a woman with lust for her has already committed adultery with her in his heart.

**29** So if your right eye causes you to sin, tear it out and throw it away. It is better for you to lose one of your body parts than to have your whole body thrown into hell.  <sup>&#123;Lit. Gehenna ; a Gk. transliteration of the Heb. for Valley of Hinnom&#125;</sup>

**30** And if your right hand causes you to sin, cut it off and throw it away from you. It is better for you to lose one of your body parts than to have your whole body go into hell."  <sup>&#123;Lit. Gehenna ; a Gk. transliteration of the Heb. for Valley of Hinnom&#125;</sup>

**31** <sup>&#123;Teaching about Divorce&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Matthew 19:1-12; Mark 10:1-12; Luke 16:18)&#125;</sup> d "It was also said, «Whoever divorces his wife must give her a written notice of divorce.' <sup>&#123;Deut 24:1, 3&#125;</sup>

**32** But I say to you, any man who divorces his wife, except for sexual immorality, causes her to commit adultery, and whoever marries a divorced woman commits adultery."

**33** <sup>&#123;Teaching about Oaths&#125;</sup> d "Again, you have heard that it was told those who lived long ago, «You must not swear an oath falsely,'but, «You must fulfill your oaths to the Lord.' <sup>&#123;Lev 19:12; Num 30:2; Deut 23:21-23&#125;</sup>

**34** But I tell you not to swear at all, neither by heaven, because it is God's throne,

**35** nor by the earth, because it is his footstool, nor by Jerusalem, because it is the city of the Great King.

**36** Nor should you swear by your head, because you cannot make one hair white or black.

**37** Instead, let your message be «Yes'for «Yes'and «No'for «No.'Anything more than that comes from the evil one."

**38** <sup>&#123;Teaching about Retaliation&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Luke 6:29-30)&#125;</sup> d "You have heard that it was said, «An eye for an eye and a tooth for a tooth.' <sup>&#123;Exod 21:24; Lev 24:20; Deut 19:21&#125;</sup>

**39** But I tell you not to resist an evildoer. On the contrary, whoever slaps you on the right cheek, turn the other to him as well.

**40** If anyone wants to sue you and take your shirt, let him have your coat as well.

**41** And if anyone forces you to go one mile, go two with him.

**42** Give to the person who asks you for something, and do not turn away from the person who wants to borrow something from you."

**43** <sup>&#123;Teaching about Love for Enemies&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Luke 6:27-28, 32-36)&#125;</sup> d "You have heard that it was said, «You must love your neighbor' <sup>&#123;Lev 19:18&#125;</sup> and hate your enemy.

**44** But I say to you, love your enemies, and pray for those who persecute you,

**45** so that you will become children of your Father in heaven, because he makes his sun rise on both evil and good people, and he lets rain fall on the righteous and the unrighteous.

**46** If you love those who love you, what reward will you have? Even the tax collectors do the same, don't they?

**47** And if you greet only your relatives, that's no great thing you're doing, is it? Even the unbelievers <sup>&#123;Lit. to the gentiles ; i.e. unbelieving non-Jews; other mss. read the tax collectors&#125;</sup> do the same, don't they?

**48** So be perfect, <sup>&#123;Or mature&#125;</sup> as your heavenly Father is perfect." <sup>&#123;Or mature&#125;</sup>

## Chapter 6

**1** <sup>&#123;Teaching about Giving to the Poor&#125;</sup> d "Be careful not to practice your righteousness in front of people in order to be noticed by them. If you do, you will have no reward from your Father in heaven .

**2** So whenever you give to the poor, don't blow a trumpet before you like the hypocrites do in the synagogues and in the streets so that they will be praised by people. I tell all of you <sup>&#123;The Gk. pronoun you is pl.&#125;</sup> with certainty, they have their full reward!

**3** But when you give to the poor, don't let your left hand know what your right hand is doing,

**4** so that your giving may be done in secret. And your Father who sees in secret will reward you."  <sup>&#123;Other mss. read reward you openly&#125;</sup>

**5** <sup>&#123;Teaching about Prayer&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Luke 11:2-4)&#125;</sup> d "And whenever you pray, don't be like the hypocrites who love to stand in the synagogues and on the street corners so that they will be seen by people. I tell all of you <sup>&#123;The Gk. pronoun you is pl.&#125;</sup> with certainty, they have their full reward!

**6** But whenever you pray, go into your room, close the door, and pray to your Father who is hidden. And your Father who sees from the hidden place will reward you.  <sup>&#123;Other mss. read reward you openly&#125;</sup>

**7** "When you are praying, don't say meaningless things <sup>&#123;Or words&#125;</sup> like the unbelievers <sup>&#123;Lit. gentiles ; i.e. unbelieving non-Jews&#125;</sup> do, because they think they will be heard by being so wordy.

**8** Don't be like them, because your Father knows what you need before you ask him.

**9** Therefore, this is how you should pray: «Our Father in heaven, may your name be kept holy.

**10** May your kingdom come. May your will be done, on earth as it is in heaven.

**11** Give us today our daily bread,

**12** and forgive us our sins,  <sup>&#123;Or debts&#125;</sup> as we have forgiven those who have sinned against us. <sup>&#123;Or forgiven our debtors&#125;</sup>

**13** And never bring us into temptation, but deliver us from the evil one.' <sup>&#123;Other mss. read evil one. For yours is the kingdom and the power and the glory forever. Amen.&#125;</sup>

**14** Because if you forgive people their offenses, your heavenly Father will also forgive you.

**15** But if you do not forgive people their offenses, <sup>&#123;Other mss. lack their offenses&#125;</sup> your Father will not forgive your offenses."

**16** <sup>&#123;Teaching about Fasting&#125;</sup> d "Whenever you fast, don't be gloomy like the hypocrites, because they put on sad faces to show others they are fasting. I tell all of you <sup>&#123;The Gk. pronoun you is pl.&#125;</sup> with certainty, they have their full reward!

**17** But when you fast, put oil on <sup>&#123;Or anoint&#125;</sup> your head and wash your face,

**18** so that your fasting will not be noticed by others but by your Father who is in the hidden place. And your Father who watches from the hidden place will reward you."  <sup>&#123;Other mss. read reward you openly&#125;</sup>

**19** <sup>&#123;Teaching about Treasures&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Luke 12:33-34)&#125;</sup> d "Stop storing up treasures for yourselves on earth, where moths and rust destroy and where thieves break in and steal.

**20** But keep on storing up treasures for yourselves in heaven, where moths and rust do not destroy and where thieves do not break in and steal,

**21** because where your treasure is, there your heart will be also."

**22** <sup>&#123;The Lamp of the Body&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Luke 11:34-36)&#125;</sup> d "The eye is the lamp of the body. So if your eye is healthy, your whole body will be full of light.

**23** But if your eye is evil, your whole body will be full of darkness. Therefore, if the light within you has turned into darkness, how great is that darkness!"

**24** <sup>&#123;God and Riches&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Luke 16:13)&#125;</sup> d "No one can serve two masters, because either he will hate one and love the other, or be loyal to one and despise the other. You cannot serve God and riches!" <sup>&#123;Lit. mammon ; i.e. wealth&#125;</sup>

**25** <sup>&#123;Stop Worrying&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Luke 12:22-34)&#125;</sup> d "That's why I'm telling you to stop worrying about your life what you will eat or what you will drink <sup>&#123;Other mss. lack or what you will drink&#125;</sup> or about your body what you will wear. Life is more than food, isn't it, and the body more than clothing?

**26** Look at the birds in the sky. They don't plant or harvest or gather food into barns, and yet your heavenly Father feeds them. You are more valuable than they are, aren't you?

**27** Can any of you add a single hour to the length of your life <sup>&#123;Or add one cubit to your height&#125;</sup> by worrying?

**28** And why do you worry about clothes? Consider the lilies in the field and how they grow. They don't work or spin yarn,

**29** but I tell you that not even Solomon in all of his splendor was clothed like one of them.

**30** Now if that is the way God clothes the grass in the field, which is alive today and thrown into an oven tomorrow, won't he clothe you much better you who have little faith?

**31** "So don't ever worry by saying, «What are we going to eat? 'or «What are we going to drink? 'or «What are we going to wear?»

**32** because it is the unbelievers <sup>&#123;Lit. gentiles ; i.e. unbelieving non-Jews&#125;</sup> who are eager for all those things. Surely your heavenly Father knows that you need all of them!

**33** But first be concerned about God's kingdom and his righteousness, <sup>&#123;Other mss. read his kingdom and righteousness&#125;</sup> and all of these things will be provided for you as well.

**34** So never worry about tomorrow, because tomorrow will worry about itself. Each day has enough trouble of its own."

## Chapter 7

**1** <sup>&#123;Judging Others&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Luke 6:37-38, 41-42)&#125;</sup> d "Stop judging, so that you won't be judged,

**2** because the way that you judge <sup>&#123;Lit. measure&#125;</sup> others will be the way that you will be judged, and you will be evaluated by the standard with which you evaluate others.

**3** "Why do you see the speck in your brother's eye but fail to notice the beam in your own eye?

**4** Or how can you say to your brother, «Let me take the speck out of your eye,'when the beam is in your own eye?

**5** You hypocrite! First remove the beam from your own eye, and then you will see clearly enough to remove the speck from your brother's eye."

**6** <sup>&#123;Despising the Holy&#125;</sup> d "Never give what is holy to dogs or throw your pearls before pigs. Otherwise, they will trample them with their feet and then turn around and attack you."

**7** <sup>&#123;Ask, Search, Knock&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Luke 11:9-13)&#125;</sup> d "Keep asking, and it will be given to you. Keep searching, and you will find. Keep knocking, and the door <sup>&#123;Lit. and it&#125;</sup> will be opened for you.

**8** Because everyone who keeps asking will receive, and the person who keeps searching will find, and the person who keeps knocking will have the door <sup>&#123;Lit. have it&#125;</sup> opened.

**9** "There isn't a person among you who would give his son a stone if he asked for bread, is there?

**10** Or if he asks for a fish, he wouldn't give him a snake, would he?

**11** So if you who are evil know how to give good gifts to your children, how much more will your Father in heaven give good things to those who keep on asking him!

**12** Therefore, whatever you want people to do for you, do the same for them, because this summarizes the Law and the Prophets."

**13** <sup>&#123;The Narrow Gate&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Luke 13:24)&#125;</sup> d "Go in through the narrow gate, because the gate is wide and the road is spacious that leads to destruction, and many people are entering by it.

**14** How narrow is the gate and how constricted is the road that leads to life, and there aren't many people who find it!"

**15** <sup>&#123;A Tree is Known by Its Fruit&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Luke 6:43-44)&#125;</sup> d "Beware of false prophets who come to you in sheep's clothing but inwardly are savage wolves.

**16** You will know them by their fruit. Grapes aren't gathered from thorns, or figs from thistles, are they?

**17** In the same way, every good tree produces good fruit, but a rotten tree produces bad fruit.

**18** A good tree cannot produce bad fruit, and a rotten tree cannot produce good fruit.

**19** Every tree that doesn't produce good fruit will be cut down and thrown into a fire.

**20** So by their fruit you will know them."

**21** <sup>&#123;I Never Knew You&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Luke 6:46; 13:25-27)&#125;</sup> d "Not everyone who keeps saying to me, «Lord, Lord,'will get into the kingdom from <sup>&#123;Lit. of&#125;</sup> heaven, but only the person who keeps doing the will of my Father in heaven.

**22** Many will say to me on that day, «Lord, Lord, we prophesied in your name, drove out demons in your name, and performed many miracles in your name, didn't we?»

**23** Then I will tell them plainly, «I never knew you. Get away from me, you who practice evil!»"  <sup>&#123;Ps 6:8&#125;</sup>

**24** <sup>&#123;The Two Foundations&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Luke 6:47-49)&#125;</sup> d "Therefore, everyone who listens to these messages <sup>&#123;Or words&#125;</sup> of mine and puts them into practice is like a wise man who built his house on a rock.

**25** The rain fell, the floods came, and the winds blew and beat against that house, but it did not collapse because its foundation was on the rock.

**26** "Everyone who keeps on hearing these messages <sup>&#123;Or words&#125;</sup> of mine and never puts them into practice is like a foolish man who built his house on sand.

**27** The rain fell, the floods came, and the winds blew and battered that house, and it collapsed, and its collapse was total."

**28** When Jesus had finished saying all these things, <sup>&#123;Lit. finished all these sayings&#125;</sup> the crowds were utterly amazed at his teaching,

**29** because he was teaching them like a person who had authority, and not like their scribes.

## Chapter 8

**1** <sup>&#123;Jesus Cleanses a Leper&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Mark 1:40-45; Luke 5:12-16)&#125;</sup> d When Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> came down from the hillside, large crowds followed him.

**2** Suddenly, a leper <sup>&#123;I.e. a man with a serious skin disease&#125;</sup> came up to him, fell down before him, and said, "Sir, <sup>&#123;Or Lord&#125;</sup> if you want to, you can make me clean." <sup>&#123;I.e. restored to health and qualified to participate in worship&#125;</sup>

**3** So Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. He&#125;</sup> reached out his hand, touched him, and said, "I do want to. Be clean!" And instantly his leprosy was made clean.

**4** Then Jesus told him, "See to it that you don't speak to anyone. Instead, go and show yourself to the priest, and then offer the sacrifice that Moses commanded <sup>&#123;Lev 14:3-32&#125;</sup> as proof to the authorities." <sup>&#123;Lit. to them&#125;</sup>

**5** <sup>&#123;Jesus Heals a Centurion's Servant&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Luke 7:1-10; John 4:43-54)&#125;</sup> d When Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> returned to Capernaum, a centurion <sup>&#123;I.e. a commander of about 100 soldiers&#125;</sup> came up to him and begged him repeatedly,

**6** "Sir, <sup>&#123;Or Lord&#125;</sup> my servant is lying at home paralyzed and in terrible pain."

**7** Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. He&#125;</sup> told him, "I will come and heal him."

**8** The centurion replied, "Sir, <sup>&#123;Or Lord&#125;</sup> I am not worthy to have you come under my roof. But just say the word, and my servant will be healed,

**9** because I, too, am a man under authority and I have soldiers under me. I say to one of them <sup>&#123;The Gk. lacks of them&#125;</sup> «Go'and he goes, to another «Come'and he comes, and to my servant «Do this'and he does it."

**10** When Jesus heard this, he was amazed and told those who were following him, "I tell all of you <sup>&#123;The Gk. pronoun you is pl.&#125;</sup> with certainty, not even <sup>&#123;Other mss. read in no one&#125;</sup> in Israel have I found this kind of faith!

**11** I tell all of you, <sup>&#123;The Gk. pronoun you is pl.&#125;</sup> many will come from east and west and will feast with Abraham, Isaac, and Jacob in the kingdom from <sup>&#123;Lit. of&#125;</sup> heaven.

**12** But the unfaithful heirs  <sup>&#123;Lit. the sons&#125;</sup> of that kingdom will be thrown into the darkness outside. In that place there will be wailing and gnashing of teeth." <sup>&#123;I.e. extreme pain&#125;</sup>

**13** "Go," Jesus told the centurion, "and it will be done for you, just as you have believed." And his servant was healed that very hour.

**14** <sup>&#123;Jesus Heals Many People&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Mark 1:29-34; Luke 4:38-41)&#125;</sup> d When Jesus went into Peter's house, he saw Peter's <sup>&#123;Lit. his&#125;</sup> mother-in-law lying in bed, sick with a fever.

**15** He touched her hand, and the fever left her. Then she got up and began serving him.

**16** When evening came, people <sup>&#123;Lit. they&#125;</sup> brought to him many who were possessed by demons. He drove out the spirits by speaking a command <sup>&#123;Lit. spirits with a word&#125;</sup> and healed everyone who was sick.

**17** This was to fulfill what was declared by the prophet Isaiah when he said, "It was he who took our illnesses away and removed our diseases." <sup>&#123;Isa 53:4&#125;</sup>

**18** <sup>&#123;The Would-be Followers of Jesus&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Luke 9:57-62)&#125;</sup> d When Jesus saw the large crowds around him, he gave orders to cross to the other side of the Sea of Galilee. <sup>&#123;The Gk. lacks of the Sea of Galilee&#125;</sup>

**19** Just then, a scribe came up and told him, "Teacher, I will follow you wherever you go."

**20** Jesus told him, "Foxes have holes and birds <sup>&#123;Lit. birds in the sky&#125;</sup> have nests, but the Son of Man has no place to rest." <sup>&#123;Lit. no place to lay his head&#125;</sup>

**21** Then another of his disciples told him, "Lord, <sup>&#123;Or Sir&#125;</sup> first let me go and bury my father."

**22** But Jesus told him, "Follow me, and let the dead bury their own dead."

**23** <sup>&#123;Jesus Calms the Sea&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Mark 4:35-41; Luke 8:22-25)&#125;</sup> d When Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> got into the boat, his disciples went with him.

**24** Suddenly, a violent storm came up on the sea, so that the boat began to be swamped by the waves. Yet Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> kept sleeping.

**25** They <sup>&#123;Other mss. read The disciples&#125;</sup> went to him and woke him up. "Lord!" they cried, "Save us! We're going to die!"

**26** He asked them, "Why are you afraid, you who have little faith?" Then he got up and rebuked the winds and the sea, and there was a great calm.

**27** The men were amazed. "What kind of man is this?" they asked. "Even the winds and the sea obey him!"

**28** <sup>&#123;Jesus Heals Two Demon-Possessed Men&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Mark 5:1-20; Luke 8:26-39)&#125;</sup> d When Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> arrived on the other side in the region of the Gerasenes, <sup>&#123;Other mss. read Gergesenes ; still other mss. read Gadarenes&#125;</sup> two demon-possessed men met him as they were coming out of the tombs. They were so violent that no one could travel on that road.

**29** Suddenly, they screamed, "What do you want with us, Son of God? Did you come here to torture us before the proper time?"

**30** Now a large herd of pigs was grazing some distance away from them.

**31** So the demons began to plead with Jesus, <sup>&#123;Lit. him&#125;</sup> saying, "If you drive us out, send us into that herd of pigs."

**32** He told them, "Go," and they came out and went into the pigs. Suddenly, the whole herd rushed down the cliff into the sea and drowned in the water.

**33** Now when those who had been taking care of the pigs ran away, they came into the city and reported everything, especially what had happened to the demon-possessed men.

**34** Then the whole city went out to meet Jesus, and as soon as they saw him, they begged him to leave their region.

## Chapter 9

**1** <sup>&#123;Jesus Heals a Paralyzed Man&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Mark 2:1-12; Luke 5:17-26)&#125;</sup> d After getting into a boat, Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> crossed to the other side and came to his own city.

**2** All at once some people <sup>&#123;Lit. they&#125;</sup> brought him a paralyzed man lying on a stretcher. When Jesus saw their faith, he told the paralyzed man, "Be courageous, son! Your sins are forgiven."

**3** Then some of the scribes told themselves, "This fellow is blaspheming!"

**4** But Jesus, knowing <sup>&#123;Lit. saw&#125;</sup> what they were thinking, replied, "Why do you have such evil thoughts in your hearts?

**5** Which is easier: to say, «Your sins are forgiven,'or to say, «Get up and walk'?

**6** But so you will know that the Son of Man has authority on earth to forgive sins" he told the paralyzed man, "Get up, pick up your stretcher, and go home!"

**7** So the man <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> got up and went home.

**8** When the crowds saw this, they became frightened <sup>&#123;Other mss. read they were amazed&#125;</sup> and glorified God for giving such authority to humans.

**9** <sup>&#123;Jesus Calls Matthew&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Mark 2:13-17; Luke 5:27-32)&#125;</sup> d As Jesus went on from there, he saw a man named Matthew sitting at the tax collector's desk and told him, "Follow me." So he got up and followed him.

**10** While he was having dinner at Matthew's <sup>&#123;Lit. the&#125;</sup> home, many tax collectors and sinners arrived and began eating with Jesus and his disciples.

**11** The Pharisees saw this and asked his disciples, "Why does your teacher eat with tax collectors and sinners?"

**12** When Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> heard that, he said, "Healthy people don't need a physician, but sick people do.

**13** Go and learn what this means: «I want mercy and not sacrifice,'<sup>&#123;Hos 6:6&#125;</sup> because I did not come to call righteous people, but sinners."

**14** <sup>&#123;A Question about Fasting&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Mark 2:18-22; Luke 5:33-39)&#125;</sup> d Then John's disciples came to Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. him&#125;</sup> and asked, "Why do we and the Pharisees fast often, <sup>&#123;Other mss. lack often&#125;</sup> but your disciples don't fast?"

**15** Jesus asked them, "The wedding guests <sup>&#123;Lit. The children of the wedding hall; or The children of the groom&#125;</sup> can't mourn as long as the groom is with them, can they? But the time will come when the groom will be taken away from them, and then they will fast."

**16** <sup>&#123;The Unshrunk Cloth&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Mark 2:21; Luke 5:36)&#125;</sup> d "No one patches an old garment with a piece of unshrunk cloth, because the patch pulls away from the garment, and a worse tear results.

**17** Nor do people <sup>&#123;Lit. they&#125;</sup> pour new wine into old wineskins. If they do, the skins will burst, the wine will spill out, and the skins will be ruined. Instead, they pour new wine into fresh wineskins, and both are preserved."

**18** <sup>&#123;Jesus Heals a Woman and Resurrects a Girl&#125;</sup> <sup>&#123;(Mark 5:21-43; Luke 8:40-56)&#125;</sup> d While Jesus <sup>&#123;Lit. he&#125;</sup> was telling them these things, an official came up and fell down before him. "My daughter has just died,"he said. "But come and lay your hand on her, and she will live."

**19** So Jesus got up and followed him, along with his disciples.

**20** Just then a woman who had been suffering from chronic bleeding for twelve years came up behind him and touched the tassel of his garment,

**21** because she had been saying to herself, "If I just touch his robe, I will get well."

**22** When Jesus turned and saw her, he said, "Be courageous, daughter! Your faith has made you well." And from that very hour the woman was well.

**23** When Jesus came to the official's house and saw the flute players and the crowd making a commotion,

**24** he said, "Go away! The young lady hasn't died but is sleeping." But they ridiculed him with laughter.

**25** When the crowd had been driven outside, he went in, took her by the hand, and the young lady got up.

**26** The news of this spread throughout that land.

**27** <sup>&#123;Jesus Heals Two Blind Men&#125;</sup> d As Jesus was traveling on from there, two blind men followed him, shouting, "Have mercy on us, Son of David!"

**28** When he had gone into the house, the blind men came to him. Jesus asked them, "Do you believe I can do this?" They told him, "Yes, Lord!" <sup>&#123;Or Sir&#125;</sup>

**29** Then he touched their eyes and said, "According to your faith, let it be done for you!"

**30** And their eyes were opened. Then Jesus sternly told them, "See to it that nobody knows about this."

**31** But they went out and spread the news about him throughout that region.

**32** <sup>&#123;Jesus Heals a Man who Couldn't Talk&#125;</sup> d As the men <sup>&#123;Lit. As they&#125;</sup> were going out, a man who couldn't talk because he was demon-possessed was brought to him.

**33** As soon as the demon had been driven out, the man <sup>&#123;Lit. the man who couldn't talk&#125;</sup> began to speak. The crowds were amazed and said, "Nothing like this has ever been seen in Israel!"
